repeat task.wait() until game:IsLoaded()

-- VIPER STARTUP OPTIMIZATION
-- Designed to reduce work during the initial executor/PolSec pass:
-- * no bulk intro-song downloads
-- * no per-frame mobile visual polling
-- * ragdoll handling is event-driven
-- * mobile buttons use viewport-relative defaults
_G.ViperStartupOptimized = true

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

-- TOP-RIGHT PLAYER LIST FIX
-- Hide Roblox's default player-list/leaderboard in the top-right so it does not
-- cover the game or clash with the Viper HUD. This does not affect gameplay.
_G.ViperHideTopRightPlayerList = true
local function ViperApplyTopRightPlayerListFix()
    if not _G.ViperHideTopRightPlayerList then return end
    pcall(function()
        local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
        StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList, false)
    end)
end
ViperApplyTopRightPlayerListFix()
task.defer(ViperApplyTopRightPlayerListFix)
_G.ViperDeviceMode = _G.ViperDeviceMode or "Auto"
_G.ViperIsMobile = false
_G.ViperMobileProfileApplied = false
-- VIPER: BEST FAST RESET (restored from the known-working reset implementation)
local resetCooldown = false
local resetThread = nil
local currentCharacter = nil
local deathGeneration = 0
local autoResetOnDeathEnabled = false
local resetInProgress = false
local deathCharConn = nil
local deathDiedConn = nil
local deathStateConn = nil
local deathCharacterAddedConn = nil

local function disconnectDeathChar()
    if deathCharConn then
        pcall(function() deathCharConn:Disconnect() end)
        deathCharConn = nil
    end
    if deathDiedConn then
        pcall(function() deathDiedConn:Disconnect() end)
        deathDiedConn = nil
    end
    if deathStateConn then
        pcall(function() deathStateConn:Disconnect() end)
        deathStateConn = nil
    end
end

local function cleanupReset()
    if resetThread then
        pcall(function() task.cancel(resetThread) end)
        resetThread = nil
    end
    resetCooldown = false
    currentCharacter = nil
    resetInProgress = false
end

local resetToken = 0

local function ResetPlayer(mode)
    local character = LP.Character
    if not character or not character.Parent then return false end

    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return false end
    if humanoid.Health <= 0 then return false end

    -- Never start a second reset on the same character.
    if resetInProgress and currentCharacter == character then
        return false
    end

    resetToken += 1
    local myToken = resetToken

    if resetThread then
        pcall(function() task.cancel(resetThread) end)
        resetThread = nil
    end

    resetCooldown = true
    resetInProgress = true
    currentCharacter = character

    resetThread = task.spawn(function()
        local attempts = 0

        while attempts < 12
            and myToken == resetToken
            and LP.Character == character
            and character.Parent
            and humanoid.Parent
            and humanoid.Health > 0 do

            pcall(function()
                humanoid.HipHeight = 1e30
                humanoid.AutoRotate = true

                local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then
                    root.CanCollide = false
                end

                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end)

            attempts += 1
            task.wait(0.01)
        end

        if myToken == resetToken
            and LP.Character == character
            and character.Parent
            and humanoid.Parent
            and humanoid.Health > 0 then
            pcall(function()
                humanoid.Health = 0
            end)
        end

        resetThread = nil

        -- Do NOT clear resetCooldown immediately. CharacterRemoving normally
        -- clears it. If the game does not remove the old character, release
        -- the lock after a short safety timeout so a later reset cannot get
        -- permanently stuck.
        task.delay(0.75, function()
            if myToken == resetToken and currentCharacter == character then
                resetInProgress = false
                resetCooldown = false
                currentCharacter = nil
            end
        end)
    end)

    return true
end

local function StopResetSequence()
    cleanupReset()
end

_G.ViperCursedInstaReset = ResetPlayer
_G.ViperAutoResetCursedInstaReset = ResetPlayer
_G.ViperInstantReset = ResetPlayer
cursedInstaReset = ResetPlayer

LP.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(character)
    if character == currentCharacter then
        resetToken += 1
        if resetThread then
            pcall(function() task.cancel(resetThread) end)
            resetThread = nil
        end
        currentCharacter = nil
        resetCooldown = false
        resetInProgress = false
    end
end)
-- ============================================================
-- AUTO RESET ON DEATH - PREEMPTIVE INSTANT RESET
-- IMPORTANT: waiting for Humanoid.Died is already too late for the
-- Instant Reset animation/path. We therefore trigger the exact same
-- ResetPlayer sequence at the final health tick BEFORE Roblox finishes
-- the death pipeline. Died/Dead remain only as emergency fallbacks.
-- ============================================================
local autoResetOnDeathActive = false
local autoResetCharAddedConn = nil
local autoResetDeathHealthConn = nil
local autoResetDeathDiedConn = nil
local autoResetDeathStateConn = nil
local autoResetDeathCharacter = nil
local autoResetDeathFired = false

local function disconnectAutoResetDeathHumanoid()
    if autoResetDeathHealthConn then
        pcall(function() autoResetDeathHealthConn:Disconnect() end)
        autoResetDeathHealthConn = nil
    end
    if autoResetDeathDiedConn then
        pcall(function() autoResetDeathDiedConn:Disconnect() end)
        autoResetDeathDiedConn = nil
    end
    if autoResetDeathStateConn then
        pcall(function() autoResetDeathStateConn:Disconnect() end)
        autoResetDeathStateConn = nil
    end
end

local function runAutoInstantReset(char, hum)
    if not autoResetOnDeathActive then return false end
    if not char or char ~= LP.Character or not char.Parent then return false end
    if not hum or hum.Parent ~= char then return false end
    if autoResetDeathFired and autoResetDeathCharacter == char then return true end

    autoResetDeathFired = true
    autoResetDeathCharacter = char

    -- Use the SAME sequence as the manual Instant Reset.
    -- Do NOT call LoadCharacter here.
    if resetThread then
        pcall(function() task.cancel(resetThread) end)
        resetThread = nil
    end

    resetCooldown = true
    resetInProgress = true
    currentCharacter = char

    task.spawn(function()
        local attempts = 0
        while attempts < 12
            and LP.Character == char
            and char.Parent
            and hum.Parent
            and hum.Health > 0 do

            pcall(function()
                hum.HipHeight = 1e30
                hum.AutoRotate = true

                local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then root.CanCollide = false end

                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end)

            attempts += 1
            task.wait(0.01)
        end

        if LP.Character == char and char.Parent and hum.Parent and hum.Health > 0 then
            pcall(function() hum.Health = 0 end)
        end

        resetInProgress = false
    end)

    return true
end

local function setupDeathHook(char)
    if not char or not autoResetOnDeathActive then return end
    disconnectAutoResetDeathHumanoid()

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if not hum then return end

    autoResetDeathCharacter = char
    autoResetDeathFired = false

    autoResetDeathHealthConn = hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(health)
        if not autoResetOnDeathActive or LP.Character ~= char then return end
        if autoResetDeathFired then return end

        -- This is the important part: at Health <= 1 we are still alive,
        -- so the exact Instant Reset sequence can run before Roblox enters
        -- the normal death/respawn path. This gives the same visual reset.
        if health > 0 and health <= math.max(1, hum.MaxHealth * 0.01) then
            runAutoInstantReset(char, hum)
            return
        end

        -- Some games jump directly from a positive Health value to 0.
        -- By the time Died fires, the normal respawn pipeline has already won.
        -- Restore a tiny amount of local Health FIRST, then run the exact
        -- Instant Reset sequence while the Humanoid is still usable.
        if health <= 0 then
            pcall(function()
                if hum.Parent and hum.Health <= 0 then
                    hum.Health = math.max(1, hum.MaxHealth * 0.01)
                end
            end)
            task.defer(function()
                if autoResetOnDeathActive and LP.Character == char and hum.Parent and not autoResetDeathFired then
                    runAutoInstantReset(char, hum)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    -- Emergency fallback only. Normally the HealthChanged <= 1 trigger above
    -- has already started the Instant Reset.
    autoResetDeathDiedConn = hum.Died:Connect(function()
        if autoResetDeathFired then return end
        pcall(function()
            if hum.Parent and hum.Health <= 0 then
                hum.Health = math.max(1, hum.MaxHealth * 0.01)
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end
        end)
        task.defer(function()
            if autoResetOnDeathActive and LP.Character == char and hum.Parent and not autoResetDeathFired then
                runAutoInstantReset(char, hum)
            end
        end)
    end)

    autoResetDeathStateConn = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, state)
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead and not autoResetDeathFired then
            pcall(function()
                if hum.Parent and hum.Health <= 0 then
                    hum.Health = math.max(1, hum.MaxHealth * 0.01)
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                end
            end)
            task.defer(function()
                if autoResetOnDeathActive and LP.Character == char and hum.Parent and not autoResetDeathFired then
                    runAutoInstantReset(char, hum)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

_G.ViperSetAutoResetOnDeath = function(enabled)
    autoResetOnDeathActive = (enabled == true)
    autoResetOnDeathEnabled = autoResetOnDeathActive

    disconnectAutoResetDeathHumanoid()

    if autoResetCharAddedConn then
        pcall(function() autoResetCharAddedConn:Disconnect() end)
        autoResetCharAddedConn = nil
    end

    autoResetDeathCharacter = nil
    autoResetDeathFired = false

    if not autoResetOnDeathActive then return end

    autoResetCharAddedConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        resetCooldown = false
        resetInProgress = false
        currentCharacter = nil
        task.defer(function()
            setupDeathHook(char)
        end)
    end)

    if LP.Character then
        setupDeathHook(LP.Character)
    end
end

autoResetOnDeathEnabled = autoResetOnDeathActive

task.defer(function()
    if _G.ViperSetAutoResetOnDeath then
        _G.ViperSetAutoResetOnDeath(autoResetOnDeathEnabled == true)
    end
end)

function doAutoTPDown(force)
char=LP.Character;if not char then return end
hrp=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart");if not hrp then return end
hum2=char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid");if not hum2 then return end
if not force then
if hum2.FloorMaterial~=Enum.Material.Air then return end
if hrp.Position.Y<autoTPHeight then return end
end
hrp.CFrame=CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X,-7.00,hrp.Position.Z)
*CFrame.Angles(0,select(2,hrp.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()),0)
hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero
end
function _clearAutoTPConnection()
if autoTPConn then
pcall(function() autoTPConn:Disconnect() end)
pcall(function() task.cancel(autoTPConn) end)
autoTPConn = nil
end
end
function startAutoTP()
autoTPEnabled = true
_clearAutoTPConnection()
autoTPLastRun = 0
autoTPConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not autoTPEnabled then
_clearAutoTPConnection()
return
end
now = tick()
if now - autoTPLastRun < 0.1 then return end
autoTPLastRun = now
pcall(function() doAutoTPDown(false) end)
end)
if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(true) end
end
function stopAutoTP()
autoTPEnabled = false
_clearAutoTPConnection()
if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(false) end
end
function runTPFloor()
pcall(function() doAutoTPDown(true) end)
end
function toggleAutoTP(on)
if on then
startAutoTP()
else
stopAutoTP()
end
saveViperConfig()
end
function _G.ViperStopAutoTPForAction()
if autoTPEnabled then
stopAutoTP()
pcall(function() if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(false) end end)
pcall(saveViperConfig)
end
end
dropBrainrotActive = false
DROP_ASCEND_DURATION = 0.2
DROP_ASCEND_SPEED = 150
function runDropBrainrot()
if dropBrainrotActive then return end
if _G.ViperStopAutoTPForAction then _G.ViperStopAutoTPForAction() end
char = LP.Character
if not char then return end
root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not root then return end
dropBrainrotActive = true
startTime = tick()
dropConn = nil
dropConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
currentChar = LP.Character
currentRoot = currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not currentChar or not currentRoot then
if dropConn then dropConn:Disconnect() end
dropBrainrotActive = false
return
end
if tick() - startTime >= DROP_ASCEND_DURATION then
if dropConn then dropConn:Disconnect() end
rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {currentChar}
rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
rayResult = workspace:Raycast(currentRoot.Position, Vector3.new(0, -2000, 0), rayParams)
if rayResult then
hum = currentChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
offset = (hum and hum.HipHeight or 2) + (currentRoot.Size.Y / 2)
currentRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentRoot.Position.X, rayResult.Position.Y + offset, currentRoot.Position.Z)
currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
currentRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
end
dropBrainrotActive = false
return
end
currentRoot.Velocity = Vector3.new(currentRoot.Velocity.X, DROP_ASCEND_SPEED, currentRoot.Velocity.Z)
end)
end


-- ========== VIPER-ONLY TAUNT ==========
function _G.ViperTaunt()
    local message = "Viper Duels on top."
    pcall(function()
        local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local chatService = rs:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
        if chatService then
            local sayMsg = chatService:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
            if sayMsg then
                sayMsg:FireServer(message, "All")
                return
            end
        end
        local tcs = game:GetService("TextChatService")
        if tcs and tcs.TextChannels then
            local channel = tcs.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
                or tcs.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("General")
            if channel then
                channel:SendAsync(message)
            end
        end
    end)
end
-- ========== END VIPER-ONLY TAUNT ==========

-- ========== ANTI-RAGDOLL (UNPATCHED) ==========
local antiRagdollEnabled = true
local antiRagdollConn = nil

local function startAntiRagdoll()
if antiRagdollConn then return end
antiRagdollConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not antiRagdollEnabled then return end
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return end
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not (hum and root) then return end
local s = hum:GetState()
local ragdolled = (
s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
or s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
or s == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
)
local endTime = LP:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
if endTime and (endTime - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) > 0 then
ragdolled = true
end
if ragdolled then
pcall(function()
LP:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
end)
for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if d:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or (d:IsA("Attachment") and d.Name:find("RagdollAttachment")) then
pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
end
end
for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if obj:IsA("Motor6D") and obj.Enabled == false then
obj.Enabled = true
end
end
if hum.Health > 0 then
hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
end
workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
root.Anchored = false
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end
end)
end
local function stopAntiRagdoll()
if antiRagdollConn then
antiRagdollConn:Disconnect()
antiRagdollConn = nil
end
end
local function setAntiRagdoll(on)
antiRagdollEnabled = on and true or false
if antiRagdollEnabled then
startAntiRagdoll()
else
stopAntiRagdoll()
end
end

-- ========== WHALEHUB LINEARVELOCITY SPEED ==========

speedLV = nil
speedAtt = nil

function destroySpeedLV()
    if speedLV and speedLV.Parent then speedLV:Destroy() end
    if speedAtt and speedAtt.Parent then speedAtt:Destroy() end
    speedLV = nil; speedAtt = nil
end

function setupSpeedLV(hrp)
    destroySpeedLV()
    if not hrp then return end
    speedAtt = Instance.new("Attachment")
    speedAtt.Name = "ViperSpeedAtt"
    speedAtt.Parent = hrp
    speedLV = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
    speedLV.Name = "ViperSpeedLV"
    speedLV.Attachment0 = speedAtt
    speedLV.VelocityConstraintMode = Enum.VelocityConstraintMode.Plane
    speedLV.PrimaryTangentAxis = Vector3.new(1,0,0)
    speedLV.SecondaryTangentAxis = Vector3.new(0,0,1)
    speedLV.MaxForce = 10000
    speedLV.PlaneVelocity = Vector2.zero
    speedLV.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
    speedLV.Parent = hrp
end

function updateSpeedLV(moveDir, speed)
    if not speedLV or not speedLV.Parent then return end
    if moveDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
        local flat = Vector3.new(moveDir.X, 0, moveDir.Z).Unit
        speedLV.PlaneVelocity = Vector2.new(flat.X * speed, flat.Z * speed)
    else
        speedLV.PlaneVelocity = Vector2.zero
    end
end

function getAnimate(char)
char = char or LP.Character
return char and char:FindFirstChild("Animate") or nil
end
function stopCurrentAnimations(char)
hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum then return end
for _, track in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end)
end
end
function backupAnimations(char)
animate = getAnimate(char)
if not animate or next(OriginalAnims) ~= nil then return end
function getId(obj) return obj and obj.AnimationId or nil end
OriginalAnims = {
idle1 = getId(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation1")),
idle2 = getId(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation2")),
walk = getId(animate.walk and animate.walk:FindFirstChild("WalkAnim")),
run = getId(animate.run and animate.run:FindFirstChild("RunAnim")),
jump = getId(animate.jump and animate.jump:FindFirstChild("JumpAnim")),
fall = getId(animate.fall and animate.fall:FindFirstChild("FallAnim")),
climb = getId(animate.climb and animate.climb:FindFirstChild("ClimbAnim")),
}
end
function setAnimId(obj, id)
if obj and id then pcall(function() obj.AnimationId = id end) end
end
function reloadAnimate(animate)
if not animate then return end
pcall(function()
animate.Disabled = true
task.wait()
animate.Disabled = false
end)
end
function resetAnimations()
char = LP.Character
animate = getAnimate(char)
if not animate or next(OriginalAnims) == nil then return end
stopCurrentAnimations(char)
setAnimId(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation1"), OriginalAnims.idle1)
setAnimId(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation2"), OriginalAnims.idle2)
setAnimId(animate.walk and animate.walk:FindFirstChild("WalkAnim"), OriginalAnims.walk)
setAnimId(animate.run and animate.run:FindFirstChild("RunAnim"), OriginalAnims.run)
setAnimId(animate.jump and animate.jump:FindFirstChild("JumpAnim"), OriginalAnims.jump)
setAnimId(animate.fall and animate.fall:FindFirstChild("FallAnim"), OriginalAnims.fall)
setAnimId(animate.climb and animate.climb:FindFirstChild("ClimbAnim"), OriginalAnims.climb)
reloadAnimate(animate)
end
function applyAnimationPack(packName)
selectedAnimationPack = packName or "OFF"
if selectedAnimationPack ~= "Unwalk" and unwalkEnabled then
disableUnwalk()
end
if selectedAnimationPack ~= "Hit Harder" and hitHarderAnimEnabled then
hitHarderAnimEnabled = false
resetAnimations()
end
if selectedAnimationPack == "Unwalk" then
resetAnimations()
enableUnwalk()
return
end
if selectedAnimationPack == "Hit Harder" then
disableUnwalk()
enableHitHarderAnim()
return
end
if selectedAnimationPack == "OFF" then
resetAnimations()
return
end
pack = AnimationPacks[selectedAnimationPack]
char = LP.Character
animate = getAnimate(char)
if not pack or not animate then return end
backupAnimations(char)
stopCurrentAnimations(char)
setAnimId(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation1"), pack.idle[1][1])
setAnimId(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation2"), pack.idle[2][1])
setAnimId(animate.walk and animate.walk:FindFirstChild("WalkAnim"), pack.walk)
setAnimId(animate.run and animate.run:FindFirstChild("RunAnim"), pack.run)
setAnimId(animate.jump and animate.jump:FindFirstChild("JumpAnim"), pack.jump)
setAnimId(animate.fall and animate.fall:FindFirstChild("FallAnim"), pack.fall)
setAnimId(animate.climb and animate.climb:FindFirstChild("ClimbAnim"), pack.climb)
reloadAnimate(animate)
end
enableUnwalk = function()
unwalkEnabled = true
char = LP.Character
animate = getAnimate(char)
if animate then
if not unwalkSavedAnimate then
unwalkSavedAnimate = animate:Clone()
end
stopCurrentAnimations(char)
animate:Destroy()
end
end
disableUnwalk = function()
unwalkEnabled = false
char = LP.Character
if char and not char:FindFirstChild("Animate") and unwalkSavedAnimate then
newAnimate = unwalkSavedAnimate:Clone()
newAnimate.Parent = char
end
end
enableHitHarderAnim = function()
hitHarderAnimEnabled = true
char = LP.Character
animate = getAnimate(char)
if not animate then return end
backupAnimations(char)
stopCurrentAnimations(char)
setAnimId(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation1"), HIT_HARDER_ANIMS.idle1)
setAnimId(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation2"), HIT_HARDER_ANIMS.idle2)
setAnimId(animate.walk and animate.walk:FindFirstChild("WalkAnim"), HIT_HARDER_ANIMS.walk)
setAnimId(animate.run and animate.run:FindFirstChild("RunAnim"), HIT_HARDER_ANIMS.run)
setAnimId(animate.jump and animate.jump:FindFirstChild("JumpAnim"), HIT_HARDER_ANIMS.jump)
setAnimId(animate.fall and animate.fall:FindFirstChild("FallAnim"), HIT_HARDER_ANIMS.fall)
reloadAnimate(animate)
end
disableHitHarderAnim = function()
hitHarderAnimEnabled = false
resetAnimations()
if selectedAnimationPack ~= "OFF" then
task.wait()
applyAnimationPack(selectedAnimationPack)
end
end

local ok, result = pcall(function() return readfile(path) end)
return ok and result ~= nil
end

_viper_writefile = writefile or (syn and syn.writefile)
canSaveConfig = (type(_viper_readfile) == "function" and type(_viper_writefile) == "function")

function getIntroSongName()
local opt = INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[selectedIntroMusic]
return opt and opt.name or "No Songs Added"
end
introPreviewSound = nil
introPlaybackSound = nil
introPreviewToken = 0
introPlaybackToken = 0
introSongCache = introSongCache or {}
introSongDownloading = introSongDownloading or {}
function stopIntroPreview()
introPreviewToken = introPreviewToken + 1
if introPreviewSound then
pcall(function() introPreviewSound:Stop() end)
pcall(function() introPreviewSound:Destroy() end)
introPreviewSound = nil
end
end
function stopIntroPlayback()
introPlaybackToken = introPlaybackToken + 1
if introPlaybackSound then
pcall(function() introPlaybackSound:Stop() end)
pcall(function() introPlaybackSound:Destroy() end)
introPlaybackSound = nil
end
end
function _safeNotify(msg)
if showActionNotification then pcall(function() showActionNotification(msg) end) end
end
function cacheIntroSong(option, allowDownload)
if not option or not option.url or option.url == "" then return nil end
if not (writefile and getcustomasset) then return nil end
local fileName = option.file or ("ViperDuelsIntroSong_" .. tostring(option.name or "song") .. ".mp3")
local function loadExisting()
if introSongCache[fileName] then return introSongCache[fileName] end
local hasFile = false
pcall(function() hasFile = isfile and isfile(fileName) end)
if hasFile then
local ok = pcall(function() introSongCache[fileName] = getcustomasset(fileName) end)
if ok and introSongCache[fileName] then return introSongCache[fileName] end
end
return nil
end
local cached = loadExisting()
if cached then return cached end
if allowDownload == false then return nil end
if introSongDownloading[fileName] then
local waitStart = tick()
while introSongDownloading[fileName] and tick() - waitStart < 12 do task.wait(0.05) end
cached = loadExisting()
if cached then return cached end
end
introSongDownloading[fileName] = true
local ok = pcall(function()
local data = nil
local req = request or (syn and syn.request) or http_request
if type(req) == "function" then local r=req({Url=option.url,Method="GET"}); if type(r)=="table" and r.Success~=false then data=r.Body end end
if not data then pcall(function() data=game:HttpGet(option.url) end) end
if type(data)=="string" and #data>0 then writefile(fileName,data); introSongCache[fileName]=getcustomasset(fileName) end
end)
introSongDownloading[fileName] = nil
if ok and introSongCache[fileName] then return introSongCache[fileName] end
return loadExisting()
end
function preloadIntroSongs()
-- Startup optimization: never download all intro songs while the script is executing.
-- Only warm the currently selected song after the UI has had time to initialize.
task.delay(2.5, function()
    if not _introEnabled then return end
    pcall(function()
        cacheIntroSong(INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[selectedIntroMusic], true)
    end)
end)
end
function makeIntroSoundFromId(soundId, name, parent)
if not soundId then return nil end
local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
sound.Name = name or "ViperDuelsIntroMusic"
sound.Volume = 0.65
sound.Looped = false
sound.SoundId = soundId
sound.Parent = parent or SoundService
return sound
end
function createIntroSound(option, fileName, parent, allowDownload)
if not option then return nil end
if option.id and option.id ~= "" then return makeIntroSoundFromId(option.id, fileName, parent) end
local soundId = cacheIntroSong(option, allowDownload)
if not soundId then return nil end
return makeIntroSoundFromId(soundId, fileName, parent)
end
function previewIntroMusic(index)
stopIntroPreview()
stopIntroPlayback()
if not INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[index] then _safeNotify("ADD SONG LINKS"); return end
local token = introPreviewToken
task.spawn(function()
local option = INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[index]
local sound = createIntroSound(option, "ViperDuelsIntroPreview_" .. tostring(token), SoundService, true)
if token ~= introPreviewToken then if sound then sound:Destroy() end; return end
introPreviewSound = sound
if not sound then _safeNotify("SONG LOADING..."); return end
sound.TimePosition = 0
pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
task.delay(15, function() if token == introPreviewToken then stopIntroPreview() end end)
end)
end
function playIntroMusic()
stopIntroPreview()
stopIntroPlayback()
if not _introEnabled then return end
local option = INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[selectedIntroMusic]
if not option then return end
local token = introPlaybackToken
task.spawn(function()
local sound = createIntroSound(option, "ViperDuelsIntroMusic_" .. tostring(token), SoundService, true)
if token ~= introPlaybackToken or not _introEnabled then if sound then pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end) end; return end
introPlaybackSound = sound
if not sound then _safeNotify("SONG FAILED"); return end
sound.TimePosition = 0
local loadStart = tick()
while sound and not sound.IsLoaded and tick() - loadStart < 10 do task.wait(0.05) end
pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
task.delay(15, function() if token == introPlaybackToken then stopIntroPlayback() end end)
end)
end
preloadIntroSongs()

_G.ViperGuiLocked = _G.ViperGuiLocked == true
_G.ViperHideMobileButtons = _G.ViperHideMobileButtons == true

_G.ViperDeviceMode = _G.ViperDeviceMode or "Auto"
_G.ViperMobileButtonPositions = _G.ViperMobileButtonPositions or {}
savedMainPositionTable = nil
savedMiniPositionTable = nil
savedStealBarPositionTable = nil
savedActiveTab = "HOME"
function udim2ToTable(u)
return {xs = u.X.Scale, xo = u.X.Offset, ys = u.Y.Scale, yo = u.Y.Offset}
end
function tableToUDim2(t, fallback)
if type(t) == "table" then
return UDim2.new(tonumber(t.xs) or 0, tonumber(t.xo) or 0, tonumber(t.ys) or 0, tonumber(t.yo) or 0)
end
return fallback
end
function collectViperMobileButtonPositions()
local out = {}
for key, entry in pairs(_G.ViperMobileButtonRefs or {}) do
local holder = entry and entry.holder
if holder then out[key] = udim2ToTable(holder.Position) end
end
if next(out) == nil and type(_G.ViperMobileButtonPositions) == "table" then
return _G.ViperMobileButtonPositions
end
_G.ViperMobileButtonPositions = out
return out
end
function keyToString(key)
if not key then return "None" end
return tostring(key):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.", "")
end
function stringToKeyCode(value)
if type(value) ~= "string" or value == "" or value == "None" then return nil end
return Enum.KeyCode[value]
end
function keybindsToTable()
local out = {}
for keyId in pairs(DEFAULT_SPEED_KEYBINDS) do
out[keyId] = keyToString(speedKeybinds[keyId])
end
for keyId, key in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
out[keyId] = keyToString(key)
end
return out
end
function collectViperKeybindConfig()
return {
keybinds = keybindsToTable(),
tpDownKeybind = keyToString(tpDownKeybind),
}
end
function applySavedKeybinds(t)
if type(t) ~= "table" then return end
for keyId in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
if t[keyId] ~= nil then
speedKeybinds[keyId] = stringToKeyCode(t[keyId])
end
end
end
function applyDefaultViperKeybinds()
for keyId, key in pairs(DEFAULT_SPEED_KEYBINDS) do
speedKeybinds[keyId] = key
end
tpDownKeybind = DEFAULT_TP_DOWN_KEYBIND
end

-- ============================================================
-- VIPER UI LAYOUT SYSTEM
-- Classic  : original 720x470 shell + left sidebar
-- Portrait : clean vertical shell + top tabs
-- Circle   : square/circular shell + top tabs
-- ============================================================
local ViperUIStyle = "Classic"
local ViperUIStyleOptions = {"Classic", "Portrait", "Circle"}

local function normalizeViperUIStyle(value)
    value = tostring(value or "Classic")
    if value ~= "Classic" and value ~= "Portrait" and value ~= "Circle" then
        return "Classic"
    end
    return value
end

local function getViperUIStyleSize()
    if ViperUIStyle == "Portrait" then
        -- Clean, narrow vertical layout. Navigation is at the TOP.
        return UDim2.new(0, 400, 0, 680)
    elseif ViperUIStyle == "Circle" then
        -- Equal width/height + UICorner(1) makes the COMPLETE shell circular.
        return UDim2.new(0, 560, 0, 560)
    end
    -- Classic is the original UI and is intentionally untouched.
    return UDim2.new(0, 720, 0, 470)
end

-- IMPORTANT: declare these before collectViperConfig().
-- Otherwise the collector resolves InfiniteJump as nil and SAVE SETTINGS fails.
local InfiniteJump = {Enabled=false,Mode="manual"}
local infJumpConn=nil
local jumpRequestConn=nil

function saveViperConfig()
    if not canSaveConfig then
        return false, "writefile/readfile unavailable"
    end

    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local mainJson = HttpService:JSONEncode(collectViperConfig())
        local keybindJson = HttpService:JSONEncode(collectViperKeybindConfig())

        _viper_writefile(CONFIG_FILE, mainJson)
        _viper_writefile(KEYBINDS_CONFIG_FILE, keybindJson)

        -- Verify that both files were actually written and can be read back.
        local savedMain = _viper_readfile(CONFIG_FILE)
        local savedKeys = _viper_readfile(KEYBINDS_CONFIG_FILE)
        assert(type(savedMain) == "string" and #savedMain > 0, "main config was not written")
        assert(type(savedKeys) == "string" and #savedKeys > 0, "keybind config was not written")
        assert(HttpService:JSONDecode(savedMain) ~= nil, "main config is invalid JSON")
        assert(HttpService:JSONDecode(savedKeys) ~= nil, "keybind config is invalid JSON")
    end)

    if ok then
        return true, nil
    end
    return false, tostring(err)
end
function _G.ViperApplyNormalInfJumpBoost(boost)
 if not InfiniteJump.Enabled then return end
 local char=LP.Character
 local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
 if root then
  root.Velocity=Vector3.new(root.Velocity.X,boost or 55,root.Velocity.Z)
 end
end

function startInfJump()
 if infJumpConn or jumpRequestConn then return end
 jumpRequestConn=UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
  if not InfiniteJump.Enabled or InfiniteJump.Mode~="manual" then return end
  _G.ViperApplyNormalInfJumpBoost(55)
 end)
 infJumpConn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
  if not InfiniteJump.Enabled then return end
  local char=LP.Character
  local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
  local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
  if not root then return end
  if InfiniteJump.Mode=="hold" then
   local jumpHeld=UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) or (hum and hum.Jump==true)
   if jumpHeld and root.Velocity.Y<30 then
    _G.ViperApplyNormalInfJumpBoost(55)
   end
  end
  if root.Velocity.Y < -120 then
   root.Velocity=Vector3.new(root.Velocity.X,-120,root.Velocity.Z)
  end
 end)
end

function stopInfJump()
 if jumpRequestConn then jumpRequestConn:Disconnect(); jumpRequestConn=nil end
 if infJumpConn then infJumpConn:Disconnect(); infJumpConn=nil end
end

function setInfJumpInternal(on)
 InfiniteJump.Enabled=on==true
 if InfiniteJump.Enabled then startInfJump() else stopInfJump() end
end

function toggleInfJump()
 InfiniteJump.Enabled=not InfiniteJump.Enabled
 if InfiniteJump.Enabled then startInfJump() else stopInfJump() end
 if setInfJumpVisual then setInfJumpVisual(InfiniteJump.Enabled) end
 saveViperConfig()
end

function loadViperConfig()
if not canSaveConfig or not _viper_isfile(CONFIG_FILE) then return end
local ok, data = pcall(function()
return HttpService:JSONDecode(_viper_readfile(CONFIG_FILE))
end)
if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then return end
savedConfig = data
local keybindData = data
pcall(function()
if _viper_isfile(KEYBINDS_CONFIG_FILE) then
local kb = HttpService:JSONDecode(_viper_readfile(KEYBINDS_CONFIG_FILE))

loadViperConfig()

-- Rebind Auto Reset On Death after config loading. The config restores the
-- boolean state, but the character listeners must also be installed.
task.defer(function()
    if _G.ViperSetAutoResetOnDeath then
        _G.ViperSetAutoResetOnDeath(autoResetOnDeathEnabled == true)
    end
end)

-- Create/update the config automatically on first execution as well.
task.delay(1.25, function()
    pcall(saveViperConfig)
end)

local function syncAnimationPackIndex()
for i, name in ipairs(AnimationPackList) do
if name == selectedAnimationPack then
AnimationPackIndex = i
return
end
end
selectedAnimationPack = "OFF"
AnimationPackIndex = 1
end
local function applySavedAnimationPackToCharacter(char)
syncAnimationPackIndex()
if refreshAnimationPackRow then pcall(refreshAnimationPackRow) end
if not char then char = LP.Character end
if not char then return end
local animate = char:FindFirstChild("Animate") or char:WaitForChild("Animate", 6)
if not animate then return end
task.wait(0.2)
OriginalAnims = {}
unwalkSavedAnimate = nil
if selectedAnimationPack and selectedAnimationPack ~= "OFF" then
pcall(function() applyAnimationPack(selectedAnimationPack) end)
else
pcall(function() resetAnimations() end)
end
end
syncAnimationPackIndex()
task.defer(function()
applySavedAnimationPackToCharacter(LP.Character)
end)
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
task.wait(0.65)
applySavedAnimationPackToCharacter(char)
end)
_G.ViperAutoResetOnMed = _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed or {}
_G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.conns = _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.conns or {}
_G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.enabled = autoResetOnMedEnabled == true
_G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.medTriggered = false
_G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.lastFire = _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.lastFire or 0
_G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.cooldown = 2.25
_G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.charAddedConn = _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.charAddedConn
-- Use the exact same BEST FAST RESET for Medusa/auto-reset triggers.
_G.ViperAutoResetCursedInstaReset = ResetPlayer
_G.ViperInstantReset = ResetPlayer

function _G.ViperAutoResetShouldFire(part)
    local state = _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed
    if not state or not state.enabled then return false end
    if state.medTriggered then return false end
    if resetInProgress then return false end
    if tick() - (state.lastFire or 0) < (state.cooldown or 2.25) then return false end
    if not part or not part.Parent then return false end

    -- Ignore tools/accessories and the character's own non-physical objects.
    if part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool") or part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory") then
        return false
    end

    -- Only a real transition to Anchored should trigger.
    -- This prevents the feature from instantly resetting just because the
    -- character already contains an invisible/anchored part when enabled.
    return part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Anchored and part.Transparency == 1
end

function _G.ViperAutoResetFireOnce(part)
    if not _G.ViperAutoResetShouldFire(part) then return end

    local state = _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed
    state.medTriggered = true
    state.lastFire = tick()

    -- Use the exact same Instant Reset implementation.
    if _G.ViperAutoResetCursedInstaReset then
        local ok = pcall(function()
            return _G.ViperAutoResetCursedInstaReset()
        end)
        if not ok then
            state.medTriggered = false
        end
    elseif cursedInstaReset then
        local ok = pcall(function()
            return cursedInstaReset()
        end)
        if not ok then
            state.medTriggered = false
        end
    else
        state.medTriggered = false
    end
end

function _G.ViperAutoResetOnAnchorChanged(part)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return nil end

    return part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function()
        -- This callback only runs after Anchored changes. We intentionally
        -- do not scan/fire immediately for parts that were already anchored.
        if part.Parent and part.Anchored then
            _G.ViperAutoResetFireOnce(part)
        end
    end)
end

function _G.ViperStopAutoResetOnMed()
local state = _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed
if not state then return end
for _, conn in ipairs(state.conns or {}) do
pcall(function()
conn:Disconnect()
end)
end
state.conns = {}
state.medTriggered = false
end
function _G.ViperStartAutoResetOnMed(char)
    local state = _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed
    if not state then return end

    _G.ViperStopAutoResetOnMed()
    state.medTriggered = false
    char = char or LP.Character
    if not char then return end

    -- Existing parts are only observed for FUTURE Anchored transitions.
    -- Do not call ViperAutoResetFireOnce here: that caused false resets when
    -- the character already had an invisible/anchored BasePart.
    for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            local conn = _G.ViperAutoResetOnAnchorChanged(part)
            if conn then
                table.insert(state.conns, conn)
            end
        end
    end

    table.insert(state.conns, char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(part)
        if not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end

        local conn = _G.ViperAutoResetOnAnchorChanged(part)
        if conn then
            table.insert(state.conns, conn)
        end

        -- If Medusa creates the part already anchored, wait one heartbeat
        -- before considering it. This avoids triggering on harmless setup
        -- objects while still catching a genuine newly-created petrification
        -- part.
        if part.Anchored then
            task.defer(function()
                if part.Parent and part.Anchored then
                    _G.ViperAutoResetFireOnce(part)
                end
            end)
        end
    end))

    table.insert(state.conns, char.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
        if not parent then
            state.medTriggered = false
        end
    end))
end

function _G.ViperEnableAutoResetOnMed()
autoResetOnMedEnabled = true
_G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.enabled = true
_G.ViperStartAutoResetOnMed(LP.Character)
end
function _G.ViperDisableAutoResetOnMed()
autoResetOnMedEnabled = false
_G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.enabled = false
_G.ViperStopAutoResetOnMed()
end

_G.ViperInstantReset = _G.ViperAutoResetCursedInstaReset

-- ========== END FAST AUTO RESET: MEDUSA / FLING ==========

function _G.ViperSetAutoResetOnMed(state, noSave)
autoResetOnMedEnabled = state == true
if autoResetOnMedEnabled then
_G.ViperEnableAutoResetOnMed()
else
_G.ViperDisableAutoResetOnMed()
end
if setAutoResetOnMedVisual then
setAutoResetOnMedVisual(autoResetOnMedEnabled)
end
if not noSave and saveViperConfig then saveViperConfig() end
end
function enableAutoResetOnMed()
_G.ViperSetAutoResetOnMed(true)
end
function disableAutoResetOnMed()
_G.ViperSetAutoResetOnMed(false)
end
function toggleAutoResetOnMed(on)
_G.ViperSetAutoResetOnMed(on == true)
end
if not _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.charAddedConn then
_G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.charAddedConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
if _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed and _G.ViperAutoResetOnMed.enabled then
task.wait(0.25)
_G.ViperStartAutoResetOnMed(char)
end
end)
end
_G.ViperCounterState = _G.ViperCounterState or {}
_G.ViperCounterState.batConn = nil
_G.ViperCounterState.batDebounce = false
_G.ViperCounterState.medConns = _G.ViperCounterState.medConns or {}
_G.ViperCounterState.medDebounce = false
_G.ViperCounterState.medLastUsed = _G.ViperCounterState.medLastUsed or 0
_G.ViperMedusaCooldown = 25
function _G.ViperFindMedusa()
local c = LP.Character
if not c then return nil end
for _, t in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
if t:IsA("Tool") then
local n = t.Name:lower()
if n:find("medusa") or n:find("head") or n:find("stone") then return t end
end
end
local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if bp then
for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
if t:IsA("Tool") then
local n = t.Name:lower()
if n:find("medusa") or n:find("head") or n:find("stone") then return t end
end
end
end
return nil
end
function _G.ViperUseMedusaCounter()
if not medCounterEnabled then return end
if _G.ViperCounterState.medDebounce then return end
if tick() - (_G.ViperCounterState.medLastUsed or 0) < _G.ViperMedusaCooldown then return end
local c = LP.Character
if not c then return end
_G.ViperCounterState.medDebounce = true
local med = _G.ViperFindMedusa()
if not med then
_G.ViperCounterState.medDebounce = false
return
end
if med.Parent ~= c then
local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(med) end) end
task.wait(0.05)
end
pcall(function() med:Activate() end)
_G.ViperCounterState.medLastUsed = tick()
_G.ViperCounterState.medDebounce = false
end
function _G.ViperOnMedusaAnchorChanged(part)
return part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function()
if medCounterEnabled and part.Anchored and part.Transparency == 1 then
_G.ViperUseMedusaCounter()
end
end)
end
function _G.ViperStartMedCounter(char)
_G.ViperStopMedCounter()
char = char or LP.Character
if not char then return end
for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") then
table.insert(_G.ViperCounterState.medConns, _G.ViperOnMedusaAnchorChanged(part))
end
end
table.insert(_G.ViperCounterState.medConns, char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(part)
if part:IsA("BasePart") then
table.insert(_G.ViperCounterState.medConns, _G.ViperOnMedusaAnchorChanged(part))
end
end))
end
function _G.ViperStopMedCounter()
for _, c in pairs(_G.ViperCounterState.medConns or {}) do
pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
end
_G.ViperCounterState.medConns = {}
_G.ViperCounterState.medDebounce = false
end
_G.ViperBatCounterSlapList = {"Bat", "Slap", "Iron Slap", "Gold Slap", "Diamond Slap", "Emerald Slap", "Ruby Slap", "Dark Matter Slap", "Flame Slap", "Nuclear Slap", "Galaxy Slap", "Glitched Slap"}
function _G.ViperFindBatForCounter()
local c = LP.Character
if not c then return nil end
local bp = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack") or LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
for _, name in ipairs(_G.ViperBatCounterSlapList) do
local t = c:FindFirstChild(name) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name))
if t then return t end
end
for _, ch in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
if ch:IsA("Tool") and ch.Name:lower():find("bat") then return ch end
end
if bp then
for _, ch in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
if ch:IsA("Tool") and ch.Name:lower():find("bat") then return ch end
end
end
return nil
end
function _G.ViperSwingBatForCounter(bat, char)
if not bat or not char then return end
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if bat.Parent ~= char then
if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
task.wait(0.05)
end
local remote = bat:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteEvent") or bat:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteFunction")
if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
pcall(function() remote:FireServer() end)
task.wait(0.15)
pcall(function() remote:FireServer() end)
else
pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
task.wait(0.15)
pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
end
end
function _G.ViperCounterIsRagdoll(hum)
if not hum then return false end
local st = hum:GetState()
return st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
or hum.PlatformStand == true
end
function _G.ViperStartBatCounter()
if _G.ViperCounterState.batConn then return end
_G.ViperCounterState.batDebounce = false
_G.ViperCounterState.batConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not batCounterEnabled then return end
if _G.ViperCounterState.batDebounce then return end
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return end
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum then return end
if _G.ViperCounterIsRagdoll(hum) then
_G.ViperCounterState.batDebounce = true
task.spawn(function()
local bat = _G.ViperFindBatForCounter()
if bat then _G.ViperSwingBatForCounter(bat, char) end
task.wait(0.5)
_G.ViperCounterState.batDebounce = false
end)
end
end)
end
function _G.ViperStopBatCounter()
if _G.ViperCounterState.batConn then
_G.ViperCounterState.batConn:Disconnect()
_G.ViperCounterState.batConn = nil
end
_G.ViperCounterState.batDebounce = false
end
startBatCounter = _G.ViperStartBatCounter
stopBatCounter = _G.ViperStopBatCounter
setupMedusaCounter = _G.ViperStartMedCounter
stopMedusaCounter = _G.ViperStopMedCounter
_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState = _G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState or {connections = {}}
function _G.ViperSetOtherPlayerCollision(state)
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
for _, part in ipairs(plr.Character:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") then
pcall(function() part.CanCollide = state end)
end
end
end
end
end
function enableNoPlayerCollision()
if _G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.running then return end
_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionEnabled = true
_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.running = true
for _, conn in ipairs(_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.connections or {}) do
pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
end
_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.connections = {}
_G.ViperSetOtherPlayerCollision(false)
table.insert(_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.connections, LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.wait(0.5)
if _G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionEnabled then _G.ViperSetOtherPlayerCollision(false) end
end))
table.insert(_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.connections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
local c = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.wait(0.5)
if _G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionEnabled then _G.ViperSetOtherPlayerCollision(false) end
end)
table.insert(_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.connections, c)
end))
local collisionScanElapsed = 0
table.insert(_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
if not _G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionEnabled then return end
collisionScanElapsed = collisionScanElapsed + (dt or 0)
if collisionScanElapsed < 0.25 then return end
collisionScanElapsed = 0
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
for _, part in ipairs(plr.Character:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide == true then
pcall(function() part.CanCollide = false end)
end
end
end
end
end))
end
function disableNoPlayerCollision()
if not _G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.running then
_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionEnabled = false
return
end
_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionEnabled = false
_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.running = false
for _, conn in ipairs(_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.connections or {}) do
pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
end
_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionState.connections = {}
_G.ViperSetOtherPlayerCollision(true)
end
function _G.ViperSafeModeGetCountdownLabel()
local ok, label = pcall(function()
return LP.PlayerGui
and LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachineTopFrame")
and LP.PlayerGui.DuelsMachineTopFrame:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachineTopFrame")
and LP.PlayerGui.DuelsMachineTopFrame.DuelsMachineTopFrame:FindFirstChild("Timer")
and LP.PlayerGui.DuelsMachineTopFrame.DuelsMachineTopFrame.Timer:FindFirstChild("Label")
end)
return (ok and label) or nil
end
function _G.ViperSafeModeCountdownNumber(text)
local t = tostring(text or ""):upper():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
if t == "GO" or t == "START" or t == "READY" then return true end
local n = tonumber(t)
return n ~= nil and n >= 0 and n <= 10
end
function _G.ViperSafeModeInDuelCountdown()
local label = _G.ViperSafeModeGetCountdownLabel()
return label and _G.ViperSafeModeCountdownNumber(label.Text) or false
end
_G.ViperSafeModeBlockedTools = {
bat=true, slap=true, sword=true, gun=true, pistol=true, rifle=true,
medusa=true, hammer=true, axe=true, knife=true, katana=true, blade=true, fist=true,
}
function _G.ViperSafeModeIsCarryableTool(tool)
if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return false end
local name = tool.Name:lower()
for word in pairs(_G.ViperSafeModeBlockedTools) do
if name:find(word, 1, true) then return false end
end
return true
end
function _G.ViperSafeModeHoldingBrainrot()
local ok, val = pcall(function() return LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") end)
if ok and val == true then return true end
local ok2, val2 = pcall(function() return LP:GetAttribute("AntiKick") end)
if ok2 and val2 == true then return true end
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return false end
local ok3, val3 = pcall(function() return char:GetAttribute("Stealing") end)
if ok3 and val3 == true then return true end
if _G.AutoCarrySpeed and type(_G.AutoCarrySpeed.IsCarryingBrainrot) == "function" then
local okCarry, carrying = pcall(function() return _G.AutoCarrySpeed.IsCarryingBrainrot(char) end)
if okCarry and carrying then return true end
end
for _, name in ipairs({"Carrying", "IsCarrying", "Grabbed", "Holding", "StealHold", "HasGrab"}) do
local v = char:FindFirstChild(name, true)
if v then
if v:IsA("BoolValue") and v.Value then return true end
if v:IsA("ObjectValue") and v.Value then return true end
if v:IsA("StringValue") and v.Value ~= "" then return true end
end
end
for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("Model") and child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) then
local n = child.Name:lower()
if n:find("brainrot") or n:find("animal") or n:find("carry") or n:find("grab") or n:find("steal") or n:find("hold") then
return true
end
end
end
return false
end
function _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked()
if not antiKickEnabled then return false end
return _G.ViperSafeModeInDuelCountdown() or _G.ViperSafeModeHoldingBrainrot()
end
function _G.ViperSafeModeForceStop(reason)
local stopped = false
if _G.ViperNormalAimbotOn and _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot then _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot(); stopped = true end
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn and _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot then _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot(false); stopped = true end
if _G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn and _G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot then _G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot(); stopped = true end
if autoLeftEnabled then
autoLeftEnabled = false
if _G.ViperSetAutoLeftVisual then _G.ViperSetAutoLeftVisual(false) end
if _G.ViperStopAutoLeft then _G.ViperStopAutoLeft() end
stopped = true
end
if autoRightEnabled then
autoRightEnabled = false
if _G.ViperSetAutoRightVisual then _G.ViperSetAutoRightVisual(false) end
if _G.ViperStopAutoRight then _G.ViperStopAutoRight() end
stopped = true
end
if stopped and showActionNotification then pcall(function() showActionNotification(reason or "SAFE MODE LOCK") end) end
end
function _G.ViperSafeModeTryStart()
if _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked and _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked() then
_G.ViperSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK")
return false
end
return true
end
_G.ViperSafeModeMonitorStarted = _G.ViperSafeModeMonitorStarted or false
if not _G.ViperSafeModeMonitorStarted then
_G.ViperSafeModeMonitorStarted = true
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if antiKickEnabled and _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked and _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked() then
_G.ViperSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK")
end
end)
end
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
task.wait(0.5)
if medCounterEnabled then _G.ViperStartMedCounter(char) end
if batCounterEnabled then _G.ViperStartBatCounter() end
end)
_G.ViperNormalAimbot = _G.ViperNormalAimbot or {conn = nil, target = nil, swingCooldown = false}
function _G.ViperFindAimbotBat()
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return nil end
for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:lower():find("bat") or tool.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
return tool
end
end
local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
if bp then
for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:lower():find("bat") or tool.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
return tool
end
end
end
return nil
end
function _G.ViperGetClosestAimbotTarget()
local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not root then return nil end
local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
if dist < minDist then
minDist = dist
closest = tRoot
end
end
end
end
return closest
end
function _G.ViperGetNormalAimbotSpeed()
if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" or currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then
return tonumber(LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 40
end
return tonumber(AIMBOT_SPEED) or 58
end
function _G.ViperGetAntiBypassAimbotSpeed()
if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" or currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then
return tonumber(_G.ViperAntiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed) or 40
end
return tonumber(_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotSpeed) or 58
end
function _G.ViperGetSelectedAimbotSpeedValues()
if selectedAimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then
return tonumber(_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotSpeed) or 58, tonumber(_G.ViperAntiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed) or 40
end
return tonumber(AIMBOT_SPEED) or 58, tonumber(LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 40
end
function _G.ViperSetSelectedAimbotSpeedValues(normalValue, laggerValue)
if selectedAimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then
if normalValue then _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotSpeed = normalValue end
if laggerValue then _G.ViperAntiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed = laggerValue end
else
if normalValue then AIMBOT_SPEED = normalValue end
if laggerValue then LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED = laggerValue end
end
end
function _G.ViperRefreshAimbotSpeedBoxes()
local n, l = _G.ViperGetSelectedAimbotSpeedValues()
if _G.ViperAimbotSpeedBox then _G.ViperAimbotSpeedBox.Text = tostring(n) end
if _G.ViperLaggerAimbotSpeedBox then _G.ViperLaggerAimbotSpeedBox.Text = tostring(l) end
end
function _G.ViperStartNormalAimbot()
if _G.ViperSafeModeTryStart and not _G.ViperSafeModeTryStart() then return false end
if _G.ViperStopAutoTPForAction then _G.ViperStopAutoTPForAction() end
if _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot then _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot(false) end
_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn = false
_G.ViperNormalAimbotOn = true
if _G.ViperNormalAimbot.conn then
_G.ViperNormalAimbot.conn:Disconnect()
_G.ViperNormalAimbot.conn = nil
end
local hum0 = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum0 then hum0.AutoRotate = false end
_G.ViperNormalAimbot.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not _G.ViperNormalAimbotOn or selectedAimbotMode ~= "Normal" then return end
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return end
local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not root then return end
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum then return end
local bat = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or _G.ViperFindAimbotBat()
if bat and bat.Parent ~= char then
pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end)
end
local target = _G.ViperGetClosestAimbotTarget()
if not target then return end
_G.ViperNormalAimbot.target = target
local targetVel = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
local myPos = root.Position
local targetPos = target.Position
local predictPos = targetPos + targetVel * 0.14 + target.CFrame.LookVector * 0.3
local direction = predictPos - myPos
if direction.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)
if flatDir.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
flatDir = flatDir.Unit
local chaseSpeed = _G.ViperGetNormalAimbotSpeed()
local desiredHeight = targetPos.Y + 3.7
local yVel = (desiredHeight - myPos.Y) * 19.5 + targetVel.Y * 0.8
if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
yVel = math.max(yVel, 13)
end
yVel = math.clamp(yVel, -70, 110)
local desiredVel = Vector3.new(flatDir.X * chaseSpeed, yVel, flatDir.Z * chaseSpeed)
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(desiredVel, 0.8)
local speed3 = targetVel.Magnitude
local predictTime = math.clamp(speed3 / 150, 0.05, 0.2)
local predictedPos = targetPos + targetVel * predictTime
local toPredict = predictedPos - myPos
if toPredict.Magnitude > 0.1 then
local goalCF = CFrame.lookAt(myPos, predictedPos)
local diffCF = root.CFrame:Inverse() * goalCF
local rx, ry, rz = diffCF:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
rx = math.clamp(rx, -2.5, 2.5)
ry = math.clamp(ry, -2.5, 2.5)
rz = math.clamp(rz, -2.5, 2.5)
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(rx * 42, ry * 42, rz * 42))
end
if autoSwingEnabled and bat and not _G.ViperNormalAimbot.swingCooldown then
_G.ViperNormalAimbot.swingCooldown = true
pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
task.delay(0.08, function()
if _G.ViperNormalAimbot then _G.ViperNormalAimbot.swingCooldown = false end
end)
end
end)
if _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual() end
end
function _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot()
_G.ViperNormalAimbotOn = false
if _G.ViperNormalAimbot and _G.ViperNormalAimbot.conn then
_G.ViperNormalAimbot.conn:Disconnect()
_G.ViperNormalAimbot.conn = nil
end
if _G.ViperNormalAimbot then
_G.ViperNormalAimbot.target = nil
_G.ViperNormalAimbot.swingCooldown = false
end
local c = LP.Character
local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if root then
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end
local hum2 = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum2 then hum2.AutoRotate = true end
if _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual() end
end
_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot = _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot or {conn = nil, swingCooldown = false, prevAutoRotate = nil}
_G.ViperAntiBypassSlapList = _G.ViperAntiBypassSlapList or {"Bat","Slap","Iron Slap","Gold Slap","Diamond Slap","Emerald Slap","Ruby Slap","Dark Matter Slap","Flame Slap","Nuclear Slap","Galaxy Slap","Glitched Slap"}
function _G.ViperAntiBypassFindBat()
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return nil end
for _, name in ipairs(_G.ViperAntiBypassSlapList) do
local t = char:FindFirstChild(name)
if t and t:IsA("Tool") then return t end
end
local bp = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if bp then
for _, name in ipairs(_G.ViperAntiBypassSlapList) do
local t = bp:FindFirstChild(name)
if t and t:IsA("Tool") then
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t) end) end
return t
end
end
end
for _, ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
if ch:IsA("Tool") and (ch.Name:lower():find("bat") or ch.Name:lower():find("slap")) then return ch end
end
return nil
end
function _G.ViperAntiBypassTrySwing()
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.swingCooldown then return end
_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.swingCooldown = true
pcall(function()
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return end
local bat = _G.ViperAntiBypassFindBat()
if bat then
if bat.Parent ~= char then
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
end
pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
end
end)
task.delay(0.35, function()
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot then _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.swingCooldown = false end
end)
end
function _G.ViperAntiBypassGetClosest()
local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not root then return nil, math.huge end
local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
if dist < minDist then
minDist = dist
closest = tRoot
end
end
end
end
return closest, minDist
end
function _G.ViperStartAntiBypassAimbot()
if _G.ViperSafeModeTryStart and not _G.ViperSafeModeTryStart() then return false end
if _G.ViperStopAutoTPForAction then _G.ViperStopAutoTPForAction() end
if _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot then _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot() end
_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn = true
selectedAimbotMode = "Anti Bypass"
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.conn then
_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.conn:Disconnect()
_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.conn = nil
end
local hum0 = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum0 then
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.prevAutoRotate == nil then _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.prevAutoRotate = hum0.AutoRotate end
hum0.AutoRotate = false
end
_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn or selectedAimbotMode ~= "Anti Bypass" then return end
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return end
local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not root then return end
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not hum then return end
if not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
local bat = _G.ViperAntiBypassFindBat()
if bat then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
end
local target, targetDist = _G.ViperAntiBypassGetClosest()
if not target then return end
local myPos = root.Position
local targetPos = target.Position
local direction = targetPos - myPos
local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)
if flatDir.Magnitude > 0 then flatDir = flatDir.Unit else flatDir = Vector3.zero end
local chaseSpeed = _G.ViperGetAntiBypassAimbotSpeed()
local desiredHeight = targetPos.Y + 3.7
local yVel = (desiredHeight - myPos.Y) * 19.5
if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then yVel = math.max(yVel, 13) end
yVel = math.clamp(yVel, -70, 110)
local desiredVel = Vector3.new(flatDir.X * chaseSpeed, yVel, flatDir.Z * chaseSpeed)
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(desiredVel, 0.8)
local toTarget = targetPos - myPos
if toTarget.Magnitude > 0.1 then
local goalCF = CFrame.lookAt(myPos, targetPos)
local diffCF = root.CFrame:Inverse() * goalCF
local rx, ry, rz = diffCF:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
rx = math.clamp(rx, -2.5, 2.5)
ry = math.clamp(ry, -2.5, 2.5)
rz = math.clamp(rz, -2.5, 2.5)
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(rx * 42, ry * 42, rz * 42))
end
if autoSwingEnabled and targetDist <= 8 then _G.ViperAntiBypassTrySwing() end
end)
if _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual() end
end
function _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot(keepVisual)
_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn = false
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot and _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.conn then
_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.conn:Disconnect()
_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.conn = nil
end
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot then _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.swingCooldown = false end
local c = LP.Character
local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if root then
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end
local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then
hum.AutoRotate = (_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.prevAutoRotate == nil) and true or _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.prevAutoRotate
hum.PlatformStand = false
pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
end
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot then _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbot.prevAutoRotate = nil end
if keepVisual ~= false and _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual() end
end
function _G.ViperToggleSelectedAimbot()
if selectedAimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn then
if _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot then _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot() else _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn = false end
else
if _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot then _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot() end
if _G.ViperStartAntiBypassAimbot then _G.ViperStartAntiBypassAimbot() else _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn = true end
end
else
if _G.ViperNormalAimbotOn then
_G.ViperStopNormalAimbot()
else
if _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot then _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot(false) else _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn = false end
_G.ViperStartNormalAimbot()
end
end
if _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual() end
saveViperConfig()
end
function _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual()
if _G.ViperAimbotSetVisual then
if selectedAimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then
_G.ViperAimbotSetVisual(_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn == true)
else
_G.ViperAimbotSetVisual(_G.ViperNormalAimbotOn == true)
end
end
end
_G.ViperNormalAimbotStart = _G.ViperStartNormalAimbot
_G.ViperNormalAimbotStop = _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot
_G.ViperAntiBypassStart = _G.ViperStartAntiBypassAimbot
_G.ViperAntiBypassStop = _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot

-- Mirror TP Down: mirror a 3-stud opponent drop while Normal, Anti Bypass, or Anti Desync Bat is active.
local MIRROR_TP_DROP_THRESHOLD = 3
local MIRROR_TP_DOWN_Y = -7.00
local mirrorTPPreviousY = {}
local mirrorTPLastTeleport = 0

local function mirrorTPAimbotActive()
return (_G.ViperNormalAimbotOn == true) or (_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn == true) or (_G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true)
end

local function mirrorTPTeleportDown()
local character = LP.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
local now = tick()
if now - mirrorTPLastTeleport < 0.08 then return end
mirrorTPLastTeleport = now
local _, yaw = root.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position.X, MIRROR_TP_DOWN_Y, root.Position.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
pcall(function() root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
end

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not mirrorTPDownEnabled or not mirrorTPAimbotActive() then
table.clear(mirrorTPPreviousY)
return
end
for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if player ~= LP and player.Character then
local root = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if root then
local currentY = root.Position.Y
local previousY = mirrorTPPreviousY[player.UserId]
if previousY and previousY - currentY >= MIRROR_TP_DROP_THRESHOLD then
pcall(mirrorTPTeleportDown)
table.clear(mirrorTPPreviousY)
if type(showActionNotification) == "function" then
pcall(function() showActionNotification("MIRROR TP!") end)
end
return
end
mirrorTPPreviousY[player.UserId] = currentY
end
end
end
end)

function _G.ViperSetMirrorTPDown(enabled)
mirrorTPDownEnabled = enabled == true
if not mirrorTPDownEnabled then table.clear(mirrorTPPreviousY) end
if _G.ViperMirrorTPDownSetVisual then _G.ViperMirrorTPDownSetVisual(mirrorTPDownEnabled) end
end
_G.ViperAntiDesync = _G.ViperAntiDesync or {conn = nil, hittingCooldown = false, h = nil, hrp = nil}
function _G.ViperAntiDesyncGetBat()
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return nil end
local tool = char:FindFirstChild("Bat")
if tool then return tool end
local bp2 = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
if bp2 then
tool = bp2:FindFirstChild("Bat")
if tool then
tool.Parent = char
return tool
end
end
return nil
end
function _G.ViperAntiDesyncTrySwing()
if not _G.ViperAntiDesync then return end
if _G.ViperAntiDesync.hittingCooldown then return end
_G.ViperAntiDesync.hittingCooldown = true
pcall(function()
local bat = _G.ViperAntiDesyncGetBat()
if bat then
bat:Activate()
local ev = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
if ev then ev:FireServer() end
end
end)
task.delay(0.08, function()
if _G.ViperAntiDesync then
_G.ViperAntiDesync.hittingCooldown = false
end
end)
end
function _G.ViperAntiDesyncGetClosestPlayer()
local hrp = _G.ViperAntiDesync and _G.ViperAntiDesync.hrp
if not hrp then return nil, math.huge end
local cp, cd = nil, math.huge
for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if p ~= LP and p.Character then
local tr = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if tr then
local d = (hrp.Position - tr.Position).Magnitude
if d < cd then
cd = d
cp = p
end
end
end
end
return cp, cd
end
function _G.ViperAntiDesyncSetupChar(char)
task.wait(0.1)
if not _G.ViperAntiDesync then return end
_G.ViperAntiDesync.h = char and char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5) or nil
_G.ViperAntiDesync.hrp = char and char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5) or nil
end
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
pcall(function()
_G.ViperAntiDesyncSetupChar(char)
end)
end)
if LP.Character then
task.spawn(function()
pcall(function()
_G.ViperAntiDesyncSetupChar(LP.Character)
end)
end)
end
function _G.ViperStartAntiDesyncAimbot()
if _G.ViperSafeModeTryStart and not _G.ViperSafeModeTryStart() then return false end
if _G.ViperStopAutoTPForAction then _G.ViperStopAutoTPForAction() end
if _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot then _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot() end
if _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot then _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot(false) end
_G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn = true
if _G.ViperAntiDesync.conn then
_G.ViperAntiDesync.conn:Disconnect()
_G.ViperAntiDesync.conn = nil
end
if LP.Character then
pcall(function()
_G.ViperAntiDesyncSetupChar(LP.Character)
end)
end
_G.ViperAntiDesync.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not (_G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn and _G.ViperAntiDesync.h and _G.ViperAntiDesync.hrp) then return end
local target, dist = _G.ViperAntiDesyncGetClosestPlayer()
_aimbotTargetPlr = target
_G.ViperCurrentAimbotTarget = target
_G.ViperAntiDesyncBatTarget = target
if target and target.Character then
local tr = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if tr then
if sethiddenproperty then
pcall(function()
sethiddenproperty(_G.ViperAntiDesync.hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", tr)
end)
end
local targetPos = tr.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0)
if (_G.ViperAntiDesync.hrp.Position - targetPos).Magnitude > 8 then
_G.ViperAntiDesync.hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
end
local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
if cam then
cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, tr.Position)
end
if antiDesyncAutoSwingEnabled or autoSwingEnabled then
_G.ViperAntiDesyncTrySwing()
end
end
end
end)
if _G.ViperAntiDesyncSetVisual then _G.ViperAntiDesyncSetVisual(true) end
saveViperConfig()
return true
end
function _G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot()
_G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn = false
if _G.ViperAntiDesync and _G.ViperAntiDesync.conn then
_G.ViperAntiDesync.conn:Disconnect()
_G.ViperAntiDesync.conn = nil
end
if _G.ViperAntiDesync then
_G.ViperAntiDesync.hittingCooldown = false
end
_G.ViperAntiDesyncBatTarget = nil
if _G.ViperCurrentAimbotTarget == _aimbotTargetPlr then _G.ViperCurrentAimbotTarget = nil end
_aimbotTargetPlr = nil
if _G.ViperAntiDesyncSetVisual then _G.ViperAntiDesyncSetVisual(false) end
saveViperConfig()
end
function _G.ViperToggleAntiDesyncAimbot()
if _G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn then
_G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot()
else
_G.ViperStartAntiDesyncAimbot()
end
end
_G.__ViperSetupNormalAutoSteal = function()
_G.ViperNormalSteal = _G.ViperNormalSteal or {
enabled = false,
radius = 62,
duration = 1.3,
animals = {},
promptCache = {},
internalCache = {},
scannerStarted = false,
scanning = false,
isStealing = false,
stealConn = nil,
refreshThread = nil,
lastSteal = 0,
cooldown = 0.08,
}
if _G.ViperNormalSteal.stealConn then pcall(function() _G.ViperNormalSteal.stealConn:Disconnect() end); _G.ViperNormalSteal.stealConn = nil end
_G.ViperNormalSteal.enabled = false
_G.ViperNormalSteal.isStealing = false
local function barProgress(p)
p = math.clamp(tonumber(p) or 0, 0, 1)
pcall(function()
if _G.StealBar then
_G.StealBar.SetState("STEALING")
_G.StealBar.SetProgress(p)
end
end)
end
local function resetBar()
pcall(function()
if _G.StealBar then _G.StealBar.Reset() end
end)
end
local function getRoot()
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return nil end
-- Ragdoll-safe root selection: Auto Steal keeps working even when the
-- HumanoidRootPart is temporarily unavailable/disabled during ragdoll.
return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
    or char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
    or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
end
local function isMyBase(plotName)
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
if not plot then return false end
local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
local yourBase = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
return yourBase and yourBase:IsA("BillboardGui") and yourBase.Enabled == true
end
local function scanPlots()
local a = _G.ViperNormalSteal
a.animals = {}
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not plots then return end
for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
if plot:IsA("Model") and not isMyBase(plot.Name) then
local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
if podiums then
for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
if podium:IsA("Model") then
local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
if spawn then
table.insert(a.animals, {
plot = plot.Name,
slot = podium.Name,
worldPosition = spawn.Position,
uid = plot.Name .. "_" .. podium.Name,
})
end
end
end
end
end
end
end
local function ensureScanner()
local a = _G.ViperNormalSteal
if a.scannerStarted then return end
a.scannerStarted = true
task.spawn(function()
task.wait(1)
while _G.ViperNormalSteal do
if _G.ViperNormalSteal.enabled then
pcall(scanPlots)
end
task.wait(3)
end
end)
end
local function findPrompt(data)
if not data then return nil end
local a = _G.ViperNormalSteal
local cached = a.promptCache[data.uid]
if cached and cached.Parent then return cached end
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(data.plot)
local podiums = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
local podium = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(data.slot)
local base = podium and podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
local attach = spawn and spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
if not attach then return nil end
for _, prompt in ipairs(attach:GetChildren()) do
if prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
a.promptCache[data.uid] = prompt
return prompt
end
end
return nil
end
local function cacheCallbacks(prompt)
local a = _G.ViperNormalSteal
if a.internalCache[prompt] then return end
local data = {hold = {}, trigger = {}, ready = true}
pcall(function()
if getconnections then
for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
if type(conn.Function) == "function" then table.insert(data.hold, conn.Function) end
end
for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do
if type(conn.Function) == "function" then table.insert(data.trigger, conn.Function) end
end
end
end)
if #data.hold > 0 or #data.trigger > 0 then
a.internalCache[prompt] = data
end
end
local function doSteal(prompt)
local a = _G.ViperNormalSteal
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or a.isStealing then return end
if tick() - (a.lastSteal or 0) < (a.cooldown or 0.08) then return end
cacheCallbacks(prompt)
local data = a.internalCache[prompt]
if not data or not data.ready then return end
