function IsClothing(obj)
for _, className in ipairs(ClothingClasses) do
if obj:IsA(className) then return true end
end
return false
end
function IsCharacterPart(obj)
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr.Character and obj:IsDescendantOf(plr.Character) then return true end
end
return false
end
function IsOutOfRange(obj)
if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
local x = obj.Position.X
return x < XMin or x > XMax
end
return false
end
function IsBase(obj)
if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
local nl = obj.Name:lower()
for _, n in ipairs(BASE_NAMES) do
if nl:find(n, 1, true) then return true end
end
return false
end
function IsInBase(obj)
local p = obj.Parent
while p and p ~= workspace do
if IsBase(p) then return true end
p = p.Parent
end
return false
end
function MakeTransparent(obj)
pcall(function()
if IsBase(obj) and not IsCharacterPart(obj) then
obj.Transparency = 1
obj.CastShadow = false
end
end)
end
function StripObject(obj)
pcall(function()
if obj:IsA("Texture") or obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
SafeDestroy(obj)
elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Sparkles") then
pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end)
SafeDestroy(obj)
elseif obj:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
SafeDestroy(obj)
elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
obj.CastShadow = false
obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
obj.MaterialVariant = ""
obj.Reflectance = 0
end
end)
end
function CleanObject(obj)
pcall(function()
if obj:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
SafeDestroy(obj)
elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
if not (obj.Name == "face" and obj.Parent and obj.Parent.Name == "Head") then SafeDestroy(obj) end
elseif obj:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
obj.TextureId = ""
elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam") then
SafeDestroy(obj)
elseif obj:IsA("PointLight") or obj:IsA("SpotLight") or obj:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
SafeDestroy(obj)
elseif obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Sparkles") or obj:IsA("Explosion") then
SafeDestroy(obj)
elseif obj:IsA("Animation") or obj:IsA("AnimationController") then
SafeDestroy(obj)
elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
obj.CastShadow = false
obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
obj.MaterialVariant = ""
obj.Reflectance = 0
end
end)
end
function ApplyGreySky()
pcall(function()
for _, obj in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
if obj:IsA("Sky") then obj:Destroy() end
end
local sky = Instance.new("Sky")
sky.SkyboxBk = ""; sky.SkyboxDn = ""; sky.SkyboxFt = ""
sky.SkyboxLf = ""; sky.SkyboxRt = ""; sky.SkyboxUp = ""
sky.CelestialBodiesShown = false
sky.Name = "_ViperDuelsNukeSky"
sky.Parent = Lighting
end)
end
function OptimizeLighting()
pcall(function()
Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
Lighting.FogStart = 9e9
Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
Lighting.Brightness = 1.5
Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(60,60,60)
for _, v in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
if v:IsA("BloomEffect") or v:IsA("BlurEffect") or v:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or v:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or v:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or v:IsA("Atmosphere") or v:IsA("Clouds") then
v:Destroy()
end
end
ApplyGreySky()
end)
end
function ApplyTerrain()
pcall(function()
local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
if terrain then
terrain.Decoration = false
terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
terrain.WaterTransparency = 1
end
end)
end
function OptimizeCharacter(char)
if not char then return end
task.spawn(function()
task.wait(0.3)
if not _viperNukeOptimizerOn then return end
for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if IsClothing(obj) then SafeDestroy(obj) else CleanObject(obj) end
end
end)
end
pcall(function()
settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Level01
end)
pcall(function() if setfpscap then setfpscap(999) end end)
table.insert(_viperNukeOptimizerThreads, task.spawn(function()
if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
OptimizeLighting()
ApplyTerrain()
for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
if not _viperNukeOptimizerOn then return end
if IsBase(obj) then
MakeTransparent(obj)
elseif IsClothing(obj) then
SafeDestroy(obj)
elseif IsInBase(obj) then
elseif IsCharacterPart(obj) then
elseif IsOutOfRange(obj) then
SafeDestroy(obj)
else
CleanObject(obj)
StripObject(obj)
end
end
for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do MakeTransparent(obj) end
end))
table.insert(_viperNukeOptimizerConns, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
if not _viperNukeOptimizerOn then return end
task.defer(function()
if not _viperNukeOptimizerOn then return end
if IsBase(obj) then MakeTransparent(obj); return end
if IsClothing(obj) then
SafeDestroy(obj)
elseif IsInBase(obj) then
elseif IsCharacterPart(obj) then
elseif IsOutOfRange(obj) then
SafeDestroy(obj)
else
CleanObject(obj)
StripObject(obj)
end
end)
end))
table.insert(_viperNukeOptimizerConns, Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
if not _viperNukeOptimizerOn then return end
if obj:IsA("Atmosphere") or obj:IsA("Clouds") or obj:IsA("PostEffect") then SafeDestroy(obj) end
end))
table.insert(_viperNukeOptimizerConns, MaterialService.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
if not _viperNukeOptimizerOn then return end
SafeDestroy(obj)
end))
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
OptimizeCharacter(plr.Character)
table.insert(_viperNukeOptimizerConns, plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(OptimizeCharacter))
end
table.insert(_viperNukeOptimizerConns, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
table.insert(_viperNukeOptimizerConns, plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(OptimizeCharacter))
end))
table.insert(_viperNukeOptimizerThreads, task.spawn(function()
while _viperNukeOptimizerOn do
task.wait(15)
pcall(function() collectgarbage("collect") end)
end
end))
end
function disableNukeOptimizer()
_viperNukeOptimizerOn = false
nukeOptimiserEnabled = false
V.nukeOptEnabled = false
for _, c in ipairs(_viperNukeOptimizerConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
_viperNukeOptimizerConns = {}
_viperNukeOptimizerThreads = {}
end
function enableCustomFont() customFontVisualEnabled=false; if V then V.customFontEnabled=false end end
function disableCustomFont() customFontVisualEnabled=false; if V then V.customFontEnabled=false end end
__viper_src_enableNoCamCollision = enableNoCamCollision
function enableNoCamCollision()
__viper_src_enableNoCamCollision()
noCamCollisionEnabled = true
end
__viper_src_disableNoCamCollision = disableNoCamCollision
function disableNoCamCollision()
__viper_src_disableNoCamCollision()
noCamCollisionEnabled = false
end

-- VISUALS: Headless / Korblox
ViperHeadlessEnabled = ViperHeadlessEnabled or false
ViperKorbloxEnabled = ViperKorbloxEnabled or false
HEADLESS_MESH_ID = "rbxassetid://1095708"
KORBLOX_MESH_ID = "rbxassetid://101851696"
KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID = "rbxassetid://101851254"
DARK_GREY_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(64,64,64)
function ViperRemoveFace(head) local f=head and head:FindFirstChild("face"); if f then f:Destroy() end end
function ViperApplyHeadlessToChar(char, enabled)
    if not char then return end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then return end

    if enabled then
        head.Transparency = 1
        head.CanCollide = false
        local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
        if face and face:IsA("Decal") then
            _G.ViperSavedFaceTexture = face.Texture
            _G.ViperSavedFaceName = face.Name
            face:Destroy()
        end

        for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and (child.MeshId == HEADLESS_MESH_ID or child.Name == "HeadlessMesh") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end

        local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
        mesh.MeshId = HEADLESS_MESH_ID
        mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(0.001, 0.001, 0.001)
        mesh.Name = "HeadlessMesh"
        mesh.Parent = head
    else
        head.Transparency = 0
        head.CanCollide = true
        for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "HeadlessMesh" then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
        if not face and _G.ViperSavedFaceTexture and _G.ViperSavedFaceTexture ~= "" then
            local newFace = Instance.new("Decal")
            newFace.Name = _G.ViperSavedFaceName or "face"
            newFace.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
            newFace.Texture = _G.ViperSavedFaceTexture
            newFace.Parent = head
        end
    end
end

function ViperApplyKorbloxToChar(char, enabled)
    if not char then return end
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return end

    if enabled then
        if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if rightLeg then
                for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") or child:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
                rightLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR
                rightLeg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Color"):Connect(function()
                    if rightLeg.Color ~= DARK_GREY_COLOR then
                        rightLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR
                    end
                end)
                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = KORBLOX_MESH_ID
                mesh.TextureId = KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID
                mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                mesh.Parent = rightLeg
            end
        elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if rightUpperLeg then
                rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 1
                local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if rightLowerLeg then rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 1 end
                if rightFoot then rightFoot.Transparency = 1 end

                local oldKorblox = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if oldKorblox then oldKorblox:Destroy() end

                local korbloxLeg = Instance.new("Part")
                korbloxLeg.Name = "KorbloxLeg"
                korbloxLeg.Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1)
                korbloxLeg.Anchored = false
                korbloxLeg.CanCollide = false
                korbloxLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR
                korbloxLeg.Parent = char

                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = KORBLOX_MESH_ID
                mesh.TextureId = KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID
                mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                mesh.Parent = korbloxLeg

                local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
                weld.Part0 = rightUpperLeg
                weld.Part1 = korbloxLeg
                weld.C0 = CFrame.new(0, -0.8, 0)
                weld.Name = "KorbloxWeld"
                weld.Parent = korbloxLeg
            end
        end
    else
        if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if rightLeg then
                for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "KorbloxMesh" then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
                rightLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            end
        elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if rightUpperLeg then
                rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 0
                local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if rightLowerLeg then rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 0 end
                if rightFoot then rightFoot.Transparency = 0 end
                local korbloxLeg = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if korbloxLeg then korbloxLeg:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end
end


function ViperApplyAvatarVisuals(char)
 if not char then return end
 pcall(ViperApplyHeadlessToChar,char,ViperHeadlessEnabled==true)
 pcall(ViperApplyKorbloxToChar,char,ViperKorbloxEnabled==true)
end
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char) task.wait(0.05); ViperApplyAvatarVisuals(char); if autoResetOnDeathEnabled and _G.ViperSetAutoResetOnDeath then _G.ViperSetAutoResetOnDeath(true) end end)

--// VIPER GRAPHICS PROFILES
-- SHINY = obvious high-quality/cinematic visuals.
-- POTATO = maximum FPS / minimum visual workload.
local viperGraphicsEffects=nil
local viperGraphicsPotatoConn=nil
local viperGraphicsSaved=false
local viperGraphicsOriginal={}
local viperGraphicsPartOriginal={}
local viperGraphicsTerrainOriginal={}

local function viperSaveGraphicsBase()
 if viperGraphicsSaved then return end
 viperGraphicsSaved=true
 pcall(function()
  viperGraphicsOriginal.GlobalShadows=Lighting.GlobalShadows; viperGraphicsOriginal.Brightness=Lighting.Brightness
  viperGraphicsOriginal.EnvironmentDiffuseScale=Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale; viperGraphicsOriginal.EnvironmentSpecularScale=Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale
  viperGraphicsOriginal.Ambient=Lighting.Ambient; viperGraphicsOriginal.OutdoorAmbient=Lighting.OutdoorAmbient
  viperGraphicsOriginal.FogEnd=Lighting.FogEnd; viperGraphicsOriginal.FogStart=Lighting.FogStart; viperGraphicsOriginal.FogColor=Lighting.FogColor
  viperGraphicsOriginal.Technology=Lighting.Technology
 end)
 pcall(function() viperGraphicsOriginal.QualityLevel=settings().Rendering.QualityLevel; viperGraphicsOriginal.MeshPartDetailLevel=settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel end)
end

local function viperClearGraphicsEffects()
 if viperGraphicsPotatoConn then pcall(function() viperGraphicsPotatoConn:Disconnect() end); viperGraphicsPotatoConn=nil end
 if viperGraphicsEffects and viperGraphicsEffects.Parent then pcall(function() viperGraphicsEffects:Destroy() end) end
 viperGraphicsEffects=nil
 for _,obj in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do if obj:GetAttribute('_ViperGraphicsProfile')==true then pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end) end end
end

local function viperRestorePotatoObjects()
 for obj,d in pairs(viperGraphicsPartOriginal) do if obj and obj.Parent then pcall(function() obj.Material=d.Material; obj.MaterialVariant=d.MaterialVariant; obj.Reflectance=d.Reflectance; obj.CastShadow=d.CastShadow end) end end
 viperGraphicsPartOriginal={}
 for obj,d in pairs(viperGraphicsTerrainOriginal) do if obj and obj.Parent then pcall(function() obj.Decoration=d.Decoration; obj.WaterWaveSize=d.WaterWaveSize; obj.WaterWaveSpeed=d.WaterWaveSpeed; obj.WaterReflectance=d.WaterReflectance; obj.WaterTransparency=d.WaterTransparency end) end end
 viperGraphicsTerrainOriginal={}
 pcall(function() for _,obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do if obj:GetAttribute('_ViperGraphicsPotatoWasEnabled')~=nil then obj.Enabled=obj:GetAttribute('_ViperGraphicsPotatoWasEnabled')==true; obj:SetAttribute('_ViperGraphicsPotatoWasEnabled',nil) end end end)
end

local function viperRestoreGraphicsBase()
 viperClearGraphicsEffects(); viperRestorePotatoObjects()
 if not viperGraphicsSaved then return end
 pcall(function()
  Lighting.GlobalShadows=viperGraphicsOriginal.GlobalShadows; Lighting.Brightness=viperGraphicsOriginal.Brightness
  Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale=viperGraphicsOriginal.EnvironmentDiffuseScale; Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale=viperGraphicsOriginal.EnvironmentSpecularScale
  Lighting.Ambient=viperGraphicsOriginal.Ambient; Lighting.OutdoorAmbient=viperGraphicsOriginal.OutdoorAmbient
  Lighting.FogEnd=viperGraphicsOriginal.FogEnd; Lighting.FogStart=viperGraphicsOriginal.FogStart; Lighting.FogColor=viperGraphicsOriginal.FogColor
  if viperGraphicsOriginal.Technology then Lighting.Technology=viperGraphicsOriginal.Technology end
 end)
 pcall(function() if viperGraphicsOriginal.QualityLevel then settings().Rendering.QualityLevel=viperGraphicsOriginal.QualityLevel end; if viperGraphicsOriginal.MeshPartDetailLevel then settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel=viperGraphicsOriginal.MeshPartDetailLevel end end)
end

local function viperAddGraphicsEffect(className,props)
 local ok,obj=pcall(function() return Instance.new(className) end); if not ok or not obj then return nil end
 obj:SetAttribute('_ViperGraphicsProfile',true)
 for k,v in pairs(props or {}) do pcall(function() obj[k]=v end) end
 obj.Parent=Lighting; return obj
end

local function viperPotatoObject(obj)
 pcall(function()
  if obj:IsA('BasePart') then
   if not viperGraphicsPartOriginal[obj] then viperGraphicsPartOriginal[obj]={Material=obj.Material,MaterialVariant=obj.MaterialVariant,Reflectance=obj.Reflectance,CastShadow=obj.CastShadow} end
   obj.Material=Enum.Material.Plastic; obj.MaterialVariant=''; obj.Reflectance=0; obj.CastShadow=false
  elseif obj:IsA('Decal') or obj:IsA('Texture') then obj.LocalTransparencyModifier=1
  elseif obj:IsA('ParticleEmitter') or obj:IsA('Trail') or obj:IsA('Beam') or obj:IsA('Fire') or obj:IsA('Smoke') or obj:IsA('Sparkles') then
   if obj:GetAttribute('_ViperGraphicsPotatoWasEnabled')==nil then obj:SetAttribute('_ViperGraphicsPotatoWasEnabled',obj.Enabled==true) end
   obj.Enabled=false
  elseif obj:IsA('PointLight') or obj:IsA('SpotLight') or obj:IsA('SurfaceLight') then obj.Enabled=false; obj.Brightness=0
  end
 end)
end

function applyGraphicsProfile(mode)
 mode=(mode=='Shiny' or mode=='Potato') and mode or 'Off'; viperSaveGraphicsBase(); graphicsMode=mode; viperClearGraphicsEffects()
 if mode=='Off' then viperRestoreGraphicsBase(); return end
 if mode=='Shiny' then
  -- Neutralize conflicting color post-processing so SHINY never becomes rainbow.
  pcall(function() for _,obj in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do if obj:IsA('Atmosphere') or obj:IsA('ColorCorrectionEffect') or obj:IsA('BloomEffect') or obj:IsA('SunRaysEffect') or obj:IsA('BlurEffect') or obj:IsA('DepthOfFieldEffect') then obj:Destroy() end end end)
  pcall(function() Lighting.Technology=Enum.Technology.Future; Lighting.GlobalShadows=true; Lighting.Brightness=3.8; Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale=1; Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale=1; Lighting.FogStart=100000; Lighting.FogEnd=1000000; Lighting.FogColor=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); Lighting.Ambient=Color3.fromRGB(175,175,175); Lighting.OutdoorAmbient=Color3.fromRGB(215,215,215); Lighting.ClockTime=14 end)
  viperGraphicsEffects=Instance.new('Folder'); viperGraphicsEffects.Name=graphicsEffectsFolderName; viperGraphicsEffects.Parent=Lighting
  local bloom=viperAddGraphicsEffect('BloomEffect',{Intensity=1.15,Size=64,Threshold=0.28})
  local cc=viperAddGraphicsEffect('ColorCorrectionEffect',{Brightness=0.14,Contrast=0.55,Saturation=0.55,TintColor=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)})
  local rays=viperAddGraphicsEffect('SunRaysEffect',{Intensity=0.32,Spread=0.95})
  local atm=viperAddGraphicsEffect('Atmosphere',{Density=0.06,Offset=0,Color=Color3.fromRGB(235,242,255),Decay=Color3.fromRGB(190,205,230),Glare=0.12,Haze=0.05})
  local dof=viperAddGraphicsEffect('DepthOfFieldEffect',{FarIntensity=0.06,FocusDistance=100,InFocusRadius=70,NearIntensity=0})
  for _,e in ipairs({bloom,cc,rays,atm,dof}) do if e then e.Parent=viperGraphicsEffects end end
  pcall(function() local t=workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass('Terrain'); if t then t.Decoration=true; t.WaterWaveSize=0.5; t.WaterWaveSpeed=15; t.WaterReflectance=0.9; t.WaterTransparency=0.1; local c=Instance.new('Clouds'); c.Name='ViperShinyClouds'; c.Cover=0.4; c.Density=0.38; c.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); c.Parent=t end end)
  pcall(function() settings().Rendering.QualityLevel=Enum.QualityLevel.Level21; settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel=Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Level04 end)
 elseif mode=='Potato' then
  -- MAX FPS: aggressively remove expensive rendering work.
  pcall(function() for _,obj in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do if obj:IsA('Atmosphere') or obj:IsA('BloomEffect') or obj:IsA('SunRaysEffect') or obj:IsA('ColorCorrectionEffect') or obj:IsA('BlurEffect') or obj:IsA('DepthOfFieldEffect') or obj:IsA('Clouds') then obj:Destroy() end end end)
  pcall(function() Lighting.Technology=Enum.Technology.Compatibility; Lighting.GlobalShadows=false; Lighting.Brightness=1; Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale=0; Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale=0; Lighting.FogEnd=100000; Lighting.FogStart=0; Lighting.Ambient=Color3.fromRGB(50,50,50); Lighting.OutdoorAmbient=Color3.fromRGB(60,60,60) end)
  pcall(function() settings().Rendering.QualityLevel=Enum.QualityLevel.Level01; settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel=Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Level01 end)
  pcall(function() local t=workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass('Terrain'); if t then viperGraphicsTerrainOriginal[t]={Decoration=t.Decoration,WaterWaveSize=t.WaterWaveSize,WaterWaveSpeed=t.WaterWaveSpeed,WaterReflectance=t.WaterReflectance,WaterTransparency=t.WaterTransparency}; t.Decoration=false; t.WaterWaveSize=0; t.WaterWaveSpeed=0; t.WaterReflectance=0; t.WaterTransparency=1 end end)
  for _,obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do viperPotatoObject(obj) end
  viperGraphicsPotatoConn=workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj) if graphicsMode=='Potato' then task.defer(function() if graphicsMode=='Potato' then viperPotatoObject(obj) end end) end end)
 end
end

local function viperGraphicsSetVisual(labelObject)
    if not labelObject then return end
    labelObject.Text = "Graphics: " .. tostring(graphicsMode)
end

function __ViperDuelsSetupVisualsUI()
Utility = pages.VISUALS
skyThemes = SKY_PRESETS_LIST or {"Off", "Night", "Aurora", "Sunset", "Galaxy", "Tech", "Sakura"}
skyIndex = 1
for i, name in ipairs(skyThemes) do if name == skyTheme then skyIndex = i break end end
function skyThemeSelectorRow(parent, order)
local row = baseRow(parent, "Sky Theme", order)
row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 42)
row.ClipsDescendants = true
local label = row:FindFirstChild("Label")
if label then
label.Size = UDim2.new(0, 92, 1, 0)
label.TextSize = 11
end
local left = Instance.new("TextButton")
left.Name = "SkyLeft"
left.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(7, 7, 10)
left.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
left.Text = "<"
left.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
left.TextSize = 12
left.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
left.Size = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 28)
left.Position = UDim2.new(1, -186, 0.5, -14)
left.BorderSizePixel = 0
left.ZIndex = 8
left.AutoButtonColor = false
left.Parent = row
corner(left, 8)
stroke(left, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.42)
local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
holder.Name = "SkyValueHolder"
holder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.28
holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 92, 0, 28)
holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -139, 0.5, -14)
holder.ClipsDescendants = true
holder.ZIndex = 7
holder.Parent = row
corner(holder, 8)
stroke(holder, Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245), 1, 0.35)
local smoky = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
smoky.Name = "SmokyBackground"
smoky.BackgroundTransparency = 1
smoky.Image = "rbxassetid://99416158073201"
smoky.ImageTransparency = 1
smoky.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
smoky.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
smoky.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
smoky.Visible = false
smoky.ZIndex = 7
smoky.Parent = holder
corner(smoky, 8)
skyValueLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
skyValueLabel.Name = "SkyValue"
skyValueLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
skyValueLabel.Text = skyThemes[skyIndex]
skyValueLabel.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
skyValueLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
skyValueLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.22
skyValueLabel.TextSize = 11

local function setSkyIndex(nextIndex)
if nextIndex < 1 then nextIndex = #skyThemes end
if nextIndex > #skyThemes then nextIndex = 1 end
skyIndex = nextIndex
skyTheme = skyThemes[skyIndex]
if applyCustomSky then applyCustomSky(skyTheme) end
if skyValueLabel then skyValueLabel.Text = skyTheme end
saveViperConfig()
end
left.Activated:Connect(function()
setSkyIndex(skyIndex - 1)
end)
right.Activated:Connect(function()
setSkyIndex(skyIndex + 1)
end)
return row
end

    local buttons = {}
    local function refreshLayoutButtons()
        for name, btn in pairs(buttons) do
            local active = name == ViperUIStyle
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = active and Color3.fromRGB(48, 48, 58) or Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 25)
            btn.TextColor3 = active and COLORS.white or Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 175)
            local st = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
            if st then
                st.Color = active and COLORS.accent or COLORS.strokeSoft
                st.Transparency = active and 0.05 or 0.60
            end
        end
    end

    local names = {"Classic", "Portrait", "Circle"}
    for i, name in ipairs(names) do
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Name = name
        btn.Text = name
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        btn.TextSize = 9
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 34)

        btn.ZIndex = 6
        btn.Parent = holder
        corner(btn, 8)
        stroke(btn, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.60)
        buttons[name] = btn

        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            ViperUIStyle = name
            applyViperUILayout()
            refreshLayoutButtons()
            pcall(saveViperConfig)
        end)
    end

    refreshLayoutButtons()
end

function ViperApplyTextColor()
local color = ViperTextColorPalette[viperTextColor] or ViperTextColorPalette.White
for _, root in ipairs({Main, PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("StealBarGui")}) do
if root then
for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
if (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox"))
            and obj.Name ~= "ViperProfileName"
            and obj.Name ~= "ViperProfileUsername" then
            pcall(function() obj.TextColor3 = color end)
        end
end
end
end
if _G.StealBar and _G.StealBar.SetTheme then pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetTheme(color) end) end
pcall(applyOverheadTheme)
if _G.ViperProfileAvatar then
    local pst = _G.ViperProfileAvatar:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
    if pst then pst.Color = color end
end
pcall(function()
    -- Keep profile text crisp for every accent, including white: color is the fill,
    -- while a fixed near-black stroke provides contrast without a neon blur.
    local crispStroke = Color3.fromRGB(8, 10, 15)
    if _G.ViperProfileName then
        _G.ViperProfileName.TextColor3 = color
        _G.ViperProfileName.TextStrokeColor3 = crispStroke
        _G.ViperProfileName.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.10
    end
    if _G.ViperProfileUsername then
        _G.ViperProfileUsername.TextColor3 = color
        _G.ViperProfileUsername.TextStrokeColor3 = crispStroke
        _G.ViperProfileUsername.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.10
    end
end)
end
function ViperApplyProgressBarScale()
local sg = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("StealBarGui")
local bar = sg and sg:FindFirstChild("StealBar")
if not bar then return end
local sc = bar:FindFirstChild("ViperProgressBarScale") or Instance.new("UIScale")
sc.Name = "ViperProgressBarScale"
sc.Scale = viperProgressBarScaleValue
sc.Parent = bar
end

bgButtons = {}
local function refreshBackgroundLabel()
    bgCurrent.Text = currentBackground == 0
        and "None"
        or ("Image " .. tostring(currentBackground) .. " / " .. tostring(#BackgroundIDs) .. "  •  tap a preview to switch")
end

function updateBackgroundButtons()
    for index, button in pairs(bgButtons) do
        local selected = index == currentBackground
        button.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.02 or 0.28
        local st = button:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
        if st then
            st.Color = selected and COLORS.accent or COLORS.strokeSoft
            st.Transparency = selected and 0.02 or 0.55
            st.Thickness = selected and 1.8 or 1
        end
        local preview = button:FindFirstChild("Preview")
        if preview then
            preview.ImageTransparency = selected and 0.02 or 0.10
        end
    end
    refreshBackgroundLabel()
end

local function makeNoneButton(index)
    -- There must be exactly one "None" entry, even if this setup is called again.
    local existing = bgScroll:FindFirstChild("NoneBackground")
    if existing then existing:Destroy() end
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = "NoneBackground"
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5,5,8)
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.16
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.Text = "NONE"
    btn.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
    btn.TextSize = 8
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 48)
    btn.ZIndex = 7
    btn.LayoutOrder = 0
    btn.Parent = bgScroll
    corner(btn, 9)
    stroke(btn, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.45)
    bgButtons[index] = btn
    btn.Activated:Connect(function()
        applyBackground(0)
        updateBackgroundButtons()
    end)
end

local function makeImageButton(index)
    local assetId = tostring(BackgroundIDs[index] or "")
    if assetId == "" then return end
    -- Never create two preview buttons for the same asset.
    for _, existing in pairs(bgButtons) do
        if existing:GetAttribute("BackgroundAssetId") == assetId then
            return
        end
    end

    local holder = Instance.new("TextButton")
    holder.Name = "Image " .. tostring(index)
    holder:SetAttribute("BackgroundAssetId", assetId)
    holder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5,5,8)
    holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.28
    holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    holder.Text = ""
    holder.AutoButtonColor = false
    holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 48)
    holder.ZIndex = 7
    holder.LayoutOrder = index
    holder.ClipsDescendants = true
    holder.Parent = bgScroll
    corner(holder, 9)
    stroke(holder, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.45)

    local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    img.Name = "Preview"
    img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    img.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. assetId
    img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    img.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    img.ImageTransparency = 0.10
    img.ZIndex = 8
    img.Parent = holder
    corner(img, 9)

    local overlay = Instance.new("Frame")
    overlay.Name = "PreviewShade"
    overlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    overlay.BackgroundTransparency = 0.72
    overlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
    overlay.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
    overlay.ZIndex = 9
    overlay.Parent = holder
    corner(overlay, 9)

    local number = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    number.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    number.Text = tostring(index)
    number.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
    number.TextSize = 8
    number.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    number.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
    number.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -22)
    number.ZIndex = 10
    number.Parent = holder

    bgButtons[index] = holder
    holder.Activated:Connect(function()
        applyBackground(index)
        updateBackgroundButtons()
    end)
end

for _, child in ipairs(bgScroll:GetChildren()) do
    if child:IsA("GuiButton") then
        child:Destroy()
    end
end
bgButtons = {}
makeNoneButton(0)
for i = 1, #BackgroundIDs do
    makeImageButton(i)
end
do
    local seenAssets = {}
    for _, child in ipairs(bgScroll:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("GuiButton") and child ~= bgButtons[0] then
            local aid = child:GetAttribute("BackgroundAssetId")
            if aid and aid ~= "" then
                if seenAssets[aid] then
                    child:Destroy()
                else
                    seenAssets[aid] = true
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
refreshBackgroundCanvas()
updateBackgroundButtons()

function stepperRow(parent, labelText, defaultValue, order, callback, minValue, maxValue)
local row = Instance.new("Frame")
row.Name = labelText
row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 21)
row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.22
row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 42)
row.BorderSizePixel = 0
row.LayoutOrder = order
row.ZIndex = 4
row.Parent = parent
corner(row, 10)
stroke(row, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1.15, 0.32)
local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
label.Name = "Label"
label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
label.Text = labelText
label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.25
label.TextSize = 11
label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -155, 1, 0)
label.ZIndex = 5
label.Parent = row
local value = defaultValue
local minus = Instance.new("TextButton")
minus.Name = "Minus"
minus.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
minus.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
minus.BorderSizePixel = 0
minus.Text = "-"
minus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
minus.TextSize = 14
minus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
minus.AutoButtonColor = false
minus.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 26)
minus.Position = UDim2.new(1, -118, 0.5, -13)
minus.ZIndex = 6
minus.Parent = row
corner(minus, 7)
stroke(minus, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.5)
local valueBox = Instance.new("TextLabel")
valueBox.Name = "Value"
valueBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
valueBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
valueBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
valueBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", value)
valueBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
valueBox.TextSize = 13
valueBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
valueBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
valueBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 26)
valueBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0.5, -13)
valueBox.ZIndex = 6
valueBox.Parent = row
corner(valueBox, 7)
stroke(valueBox, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.5)
local plus = Instance.new("TextButton")
plus.Name = "Plus"
plus.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
plus.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
plus.BorderSizePixel = 0
plus.Text = "+"
plus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
plus.TextSize = 14
plus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
plus.AutoButtonColor = false
plus.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 26)
plus.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0.5, -13)
plus.ZIndex = 6
plus.Parent = row
corner(plus, 7)
stroke(plus, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.5)
local function setValue(nextValue)
value = math.clamp(math.floor((nextValue * 100) + 0.5) / 100, minValue or 0.50, maxValue or 1.50)
valueBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", value)
if callback then callback(value) end
end
minus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setValue(value - 0.05)
end)
plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setValue(value + 0.05)
end)
return row
end
makeNoneButton(0, 8)
makeImageButton(1, 66)
makeImageButton(2, 128)
makeImageButton(3, 190)
makeImageButton(4, 252)
makeImageButton(5, 314)
makeImageButton(6, 376)
makeImageButton(7, 438)
updateBackgroundButtons()
stepperRow(Settings, "GUI Scale", viperGuiScaleValue, 3, function(v)
viperGuiScaleValue = v
viperMainScale.Scale = v
saveViperConfig()
end)
stepperRow(Settings, "Progress Bar Size", viperProgressBarScaleValue, 4, function(v)
viperProgressBarScaleValue = v
ViperApplyProgressBarScale()
saveViperConfig()
end)
speedKeybindRow(Settings, "Toggle UI", "ToggleUI", 5)
section(Settings, "MOBILE BUTTONS", 6)
section(Settings, "MOBILE BUTTON SHAPE", 7)
do
    local row = baseRow(Settings, "Shape", 8)
    local choices = {"Circle", "Rounded", "Square"}
    local buttons = {}
    for i, name in ipairs(choices) do
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Name = "MobileShape_" .. name
        b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 24)
        b.Position = UDim2.new(1, -58 - (3-i)*58, 0.5, -12)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = ((_G.ViperMobileButtonShape or "Circle") == name) and COLORS.accent or Color3.fromRGB(25,25,30)
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Text = name
        b.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        b.TextSize = 8
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.ZIndex = 7
        b.Parent = row
        corner(b, name == "Circle" and 999 or (name == "Rounded" and 8 or 0))
        stroke(b, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.35)
        buttons[name] = b
        b.Activated:Connect(function()
            _G.ViperMobileButtonShape = name
            for n, bb in pairs(buttons) do
                bb.BackgroundColor3 = (n == name) and COLORS.accent or Color3.fromRGB(25,25,30)
            end
            if _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonShape then _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonShape() end
            saveViperConfig()
        end)
    end
end

do
    local row = baseRow(Settings, "Color", 8.5)
    local choices = {"White", "Blue", "Cyan", "Purple", "Pink", "Red", "Green", "Orange"}
    local colors = {
        White=Color3.fromRGB(235,235,240), Blue=Color3.fromRGB(70,140,255), Cyan=Color3.fromRGB(0,220,255),
        Purple=Color3.fromRGB(170,90,255), Pink=Color3.fromRGB(255,80,170), Red=Color3.fromRGB(255,80,80),
        Green=Color3.fromRGB(80,220,120), Orange=Color3.fromRGB(255,150,60)
    }
    local buttons = {}
    for i,name in ipairs(choices) do
        local b=Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Name="MobileColor_"..name; b.Size=UDim2.new(0,42,0,22)
        b.Position=UDim2.new(1,-(8-i)*44-48,0.5,-11)
        b.BackgroundColor3=colors[name]; b.BorderSizePixel=0; b.Text=""; b.AutoButtonColor=false; b.ZIndex=7; b.Parent=row
        corner(b,8); stroke(b,Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),1,((_G.ViperMobileButtonColor or "White")==name) and 0.1 or 0.75)
        buttons[name]=b
        b.Activated:Connect(function()
            _G.ViperMobileButtonColor=name
            for n,bb in pairs(buttons) do
                local st=bb:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if st then st.Transparency=(n==name) and 0.1 or 0.75 end
            end
            if _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonColor then _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonColor() end
            saveViperConfig()
        end)
    end
end

stepperRow(Settings, "Mobile Buttons Size", tonumber(_G.ViperMobileButtonScale) or 0.75, 9, function(v)
_G.ViperMobileButtonScale = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 0.35, 0.30, 1.35)
if _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonSize then _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonSize() end
saveViperConfig()
end, 0.30, 1.35)
do
local row = baseRow(Settings, "Reset Mobile Buttons", 10)
local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
button.Name = "ResetMobileButtons"
button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
button.BackgroundTransparency = 0
button.BorderSizePixel = 0
button.Text = "RESET"
button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
button.TextSize = 11
button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
button.AutoButtonColor = false
button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 78, 0, 26)
button.Position = UDim2.new(1, -88, 0.5, -13)
button.ZIndex = 7
button.Parent = row
corner(button, 8)
stroke(button, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1, 0.18)
button.Activated:Connect(function()
if _G.ViperResetMobileButtons then
_G.ViperResetMobileButtons()
else
_G.ViperMobileButtonScale = 0.75
_G.ViperMobileButtonShape = "Circle"
_G.ViperMobileButtonColor = "White"
_G.ViperHideMobileButtons = false
if _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonsHidden then _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonsHidden() end
if _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonSize then _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonSize() end
saveViperConfig()
end
end)
end

_introEnabled = not _introEnabled
if not _introEnabled then stopIntroPlayback(); stopIntroPreview() end
if setIntroVisual then setIntroVisual(_introEnabled) end
saveViperConfig()
end)
end
if setIntroVisual then setIntroVisual(_introEnabled) end
end

section(Settings, "SETTINGS", 999)

-- ============================================================
-- SAVE SETTINGS
-- Saves the complete current configuration immediately.
-- ============================================================
local saveHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
saveHolder.Name = "Save Settings Holder"
saveHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
saveHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
saveHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 44)
saveHolder.LayoutOrder = 999
saveHolder.ZIndex = 5
saveHolder.Parent = Settings

local saveBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
saveBtn.Name = "Save Settings"
saveBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
saveBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
saveBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
saveBtn.Text = "SAVE SETTINGS"
saveBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
saveBtn.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
saveBtn.TextSize = 12
saveBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
saveBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
saveBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 42)
saveBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 1)
saveBtn.ZIndex = 6
saveBtn.Parent = saveHolder
corner(saveBtn, 10)

local saveStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
saveStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
saveStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
saveStroke.Thickness = 1
saveStroke.Transparency = 0.12
saveStroke.Parent = saveBtn

local saveDefaultBg = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
local saveHoverBg = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 250)
local saveDoneBg = Color3.fromRGB(28, 40, 30)
local saveDefaultText = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
local saveDoneText = Color3.fromRGB(140, 230, 160)

saveBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
    tween(saveBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = saveHoverBg}, 0.12)
end)

saveBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
    if saveBtn.Text == "SAVED!" then return end
    tween(saveBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = saveDefaultBg}, 0.12)
end)

saveBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local saved, saveError = saveViperConfig()

    if saved then
        saveBtn.Text = "SAVED!"
        saveBtn.TextColor3 = saveDoneText
        tween(saveBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = saveDoneBg}, 0.15)
        tween(saveStroke, {Color = saveDoneText, Transparency = 0.02, Thickness = 1.4}, 0.15)
        task.delay(1.5, function()
            if saveBtn and saveBtn.Parent then
                saveBtn.Text = "SAVE SETTINGS"
                saveBtn.TextColor3 = saveDefaultText
                tween(saveBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = saveDefaultBg}, 0.15)
                tween(saveStroke, {Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), Transparency = 0.12, Thickness = 1}, 0.15)
            end
        end)
    else
        saveBtn.Text = "SAVE FAILED"
        saveBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 80, 80)
        warn("[ViperDuels] SAVE SETTINGS failed: " .. tostring(saveError))
        task.delay(1.8, function()
            if saveBtn and saveBtn.Parent then
                saveBtn.Text = "SAVE SETTINGS"
                saveBtn.TextColor3 = saveDefaultText
                tween(saveBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = saveDefaultBg}, 0.15)
            end
        end)
    end
end)

resetHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 44)

local resetStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")

resetStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

resetStroke.Transparency = 0.12

local resetDefaultBg = Color3.fromRGB(232, 232, 238)
local resetHoverBg = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 250)
local resetConfirmBg = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 38)
local resetDoneBg = Color3.fromRGB(28, 40, 30)
local resetDefaultText = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
local resetConfirmText = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 80)
local resetDoneText = Color3.fromRGB(140, 230, 160)
local confirmState = false
local confirmTimer = nil
function setResetDefaultTheme()
confirmState = false
resetBtn.Text = "RESET ALL SETTINGS"
resetBtn.TextColor3 = resetDefaultText
tween(resetBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = resetDefaultBg}, 0.18)
tween(resetStroke, {Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Transparency = 0.12, Thickness = 1}, 0.18)
end
function setResetConfirmTheme()
resetBtn.Text = "CLICK AGAIN TO CONFIRM"
resetBtn.TextColor3 = resetConfirmText
tween(resetBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = resetConfirmBg}, 0.18)
tween(resetStroke, {Color = resetConfirmText, Transparency = 0.02, Thickness = 1.4}, 0.18)
end
function setResetDoneTheme()
resetBtn.Text = "DONE - REJOINING..."
resetBtn.TextColor3 = resetDoneText
tween(resetBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = resetDoneBg}, 0.18)
tween(resetStroke, {Color = resetDoneText, Transparency = 0.02, Thickness = 1.4}, 0.18)
end
resetBtn.Text = "RESET ALL SETTINGS"
resetBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
if not confirmState then
tween(resetBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = resetHoverBg}, 0.12)
end
end)
resetBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
if not confirmState then
tween(resetBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = resetDefaultBg}, 0.12)
end
end)
resetBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if not confirmState then
confirmState = true
setResetConfirmTheme()
if confirmTimer then task.cancel(confirmTimer) end
confirmTimer = task.delay(3, function()
setResetDefaultTheme()
end)
return
end
if confirmTimer then
task.cancel(confirmTimer)
confirmTimer = nil
end
resetBtn.Text = "RESETTING..."
pcall(function()
local files = {
CONFIG_FILE,
KEYBINDS_CONFIG_FILE,
"ViperDuels_MainGUI_Config.json",
"ViperDuelsConfig.json",
"ViperDuels_Settings.json",
"ViperDuels_Keybinds.json",
"ViperDuels_GUI.json",
}
for _, fname in ipairs(files) do
pcall(function()
if fname and isfile and isfile(fname) and delfile then
delfile(fname)
end
end)
end
end)

ViperUIStyle = "Classic"
viperGuiScaleValue = 0.52
viperProgressBarScaleValue = 0.83
NS = 59.5; CS = 28.8; LAGGER_SPEED = 29; LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED = 15

batCounterEnabled = false; medCounterEnabled = false; antiKickEnabled = false; autoResetOnMedEnabled = false

fpsBoostEnabled = false; antiLagEnabled = false; nukeOptimiserEnabled = false
graphicsMode = "Off"
fovEnabled = false; fovValue = 70; noCamCollisionEnabled = false; _G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionEnabled = false

if setIntroVisual then setIntroVisual(_introEnabled) end

stopIntroPlayback(); stopIntroPreview()

viperMainScale.Scale = viperGuiScaleValue
ViperApplyProgressBarScale()
applyBackground(0)
updateBackgroundButtons()
applyDefaultViperKeybinds()
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
refreshTPDownKeybind()
if stopAutoTP then stopAutoTP() end
if stopAntiRagdoll then stopAntiRagdoll() end
if normalSpeedBox then normalSpeedBox.Text = tostring(NS) end
if carrySpeedBox then carrySpeedBox.Text = tostring(CS) end
if laggerSpeedBox then laggerSpeedBox.Text = tostring(LAGGER_SPEED) end
if laggerCarrySpeedBox then laggerCarrySpeedBox.Text = tostring(LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED) end
if autoTPHeightBox then autoTPHeightBox.Text = tostring(autoTPHeight) end
if radiusBox then radiusBox.Text = tostring(autoStealRadius) end
if _G.ViperRefreshAimbotSpeedBoxes then _G.ViperRefreshAimbotSpeedBoxes() end
if type(applyCustomSky) == "function" then applyCustomSky("Off") end
if skyValueLabel then skyValueLabel.Text = "Off" end
if _G.ViperResetMobileButtons then _G.ViperResetMobileButtons() end
if _G.ViperDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates then _G.ViperDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates() end
saveViperConfig()
end)
task.wait(0.35)
setResetDoneTheme()
task.wait(0.6)
pcall(function()
local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, Players.LocalPlayer)
end)
end)
end
task.wait()
_G.__ViperDuelsSetupSettingsUI()
ViperApplyTextColor()
if ViperUpdateGuiLockVisual then ViperUpdateGuiLockVisual() end
if _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonsHidden then _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonsHidden() end
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
local isControllerInput = tostring(input.UserInputType):find("Gamepad") ~= nil
if gameProcessed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and not listeningForSpeedKey and not listeningForTPDownKey then return end
if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and not isControllerInput then return end
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return end
if speedKeybinds.ToggleUI and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.ToggleUI then
if Main.Visible then
Main.Visible = false
MiniFrame.Visible = true
else
Main.Visible = true
MiniFrame.Visible = false
Main.Size = getViperUIStyleSize()
end
saveViperConfig()
return
end
if listeningForSpeedKey then
if tick() - (keybindListenStartedAt or 0) < 0.18 then return end
local targetKey = listeningForSpeedKey
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
listeningForSpeedKey = nil
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
return
end
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Delete then
speedKeybinds[targetKey] = nil
else
for otherKeyId, boundKey in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
if otherKeyId ~= targetKey and boundKey == input.KeyCode then
speedKeybinds[otherKeyId] = nil
end
end
if tpDownKeybind == input.KeyCode then
tpDownKeybind = nil
refreshTPDownKeybind()
end
speedKeybinds[targetKey] = input.KeyCode
end
listeningForSpeedKey = nil
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
saveViperConfig()
return
end
if listeningForTPDownKey then
if tick() - (keybindListenStartedAt or 0) < 0.18 then return end
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
listeningForTPDownKey = false
refreshTPDownKeybind()
return
end
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Delete then
tpDownKeybind = nil
else
for keyId, boundKey in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
if boundKey == input.KeyCode then
speedKeybinds[keyId] = nil
end
end
tpDownKeybind = input.KeyCode
end
listeningForTPDownKey = false
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
refreshTPDownKeybind()
saveViperConfig()
return
end
if speedKeybinds.SpeedToggle and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.SpeedToggle then
toggleCarryMode()
return
end
if speedKeybinds.LaggerToggle and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.LaggerToggle then
toggleLaggerMode()
return
end
if speedKeybinds.Aimbot and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.Aimbot then
if _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked and _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked() then
if _G.ViperSafeModeForceStop then _G.ViperSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
return
end
if _G.ViperToggleSelectedAimbot then
_G.ViperToggleSelectedAimbot()
elseif selectedAimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" and _G.ViperStartAntiBypassAimbot and _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot then
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn then _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot() else _G.ViperStartAntiBypassAimbot() end
elseif _G.ViperStartNormalAimbot and _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot then
if _G.ViperNormalAimbotOn then _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot() else _G.ViperStartNormalAimbot() end
end
if _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual() end
return
end
if speedKeybinds.AntiDesyncAimbot and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.AntiDesyncAimbot then
if _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked and _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked() then
if _G.ViperSafeModeForceStop then _G.ViperSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
return
end
if _G.ViperToggleAntiDesyncAimbot then
_G.ViperToggleAntiDesyncAimbot()
elseif _G.ViperStartAntiDesyncAimbot and _G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot then
if _G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn then _G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot() else _G.ViperStartAntiDesyncAimbot() end
end
return
end
if speedKeybinds.DropBrainrot and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.DropBrainrot then
runDropBrainrot()
return
end
if speedKeybinds.AutoLeft and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.AutoLeft then
if _G.ViperSetAutoLeft then _G.ViperSetAutoLeft(not autoLeftEnabled) end
return
end
if speedKeybinds.AutoRight and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.AutoRight then
if _G.ViperSetAutoRight then _G.ViperSetAutoRight(not autoRightEnabled) end
return
end
if speedKeybinds.ViperTaunt and speedKeybinds.ViperTaunt ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.ViperTaunt then
if _G.ViperTaunt then _G.ViperTaunt() end
return
end
if speedKeybinds.AntiLag and speedKeybinds.AntiLag ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.AntiLag then
if _G.ViperToggleAntiLag then _G.ViperToggleAntiLag() end
return
end
if speedKeybinds.InstantReset and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.InstantReset then
if _G.ViperCursedInstaReset then _G.ViperCursedInstaReset() end
return
end
if tpDownKeybind and input.KeyCode == tpDownKeybind then
runTPFloor()
return
end
end)
setTab(savedActiveTab or "HOME")
_G.__ViperDuelsSetupStealBar = function()
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local UIS = UserInputService
    local TS = TweenService
    local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
    
    local existingStealBar = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("StealBarGui")
    if existingStealBar then existingStealBar:Destroy() end
    
    -- NEUE FARBEN: WEISS/HELL
    local THEME_ACCENT        = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 230)
    local THEME_ACCENT_BRIGHT = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 250)
    local THEME_ACCENT_DIM    = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
    
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "StealBarGui"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    gui.Parent = PlayerGui
    
    function drag(frame)
        local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
        frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if _G.ViperGuiLocked == true then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = frame.Position
                input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
                end)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            end
        end)
    end
    
    local pbFrame = Instance.new("Frame", gui)
    pbFrame.Name = "StealBar"
    pbFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 372, 0, 42)
    pbFrame.Position = tableToUDim2(savedStealBarPositionTable, UDim2.new(0.5, -186, 1, -92))
    pbFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 245)
    pbFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    pbFrame.Active = true
    pbFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
    Instance.new("UICorner", pbFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    
    local pbSt = Instance.new("UIStroke", pbFrame)
    pbSt.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
    pbSt.Thickness = 1.4
    pbSt.Transparency = 0.2
    
    drag(pbFrame)
    savedStealBarPositionTable = udim2ToTable(pbFrame.Position)
    pbFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function()
        savedStealBarPositionTable = udim2ToTable(pbFrame.Position)
    end)
    
    local pbScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    pbScale.Name = "ViperProgressBarScale"
    pbScale.Scale = viperProgressBarScaleValue or 1
    pbScale.Parent = pbFrame
    
    local fillRegion = Instance.new("Frame", pbFrame)
    fillRegion.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -12)
    fillRegion.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6)
    fillRegion.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 238)
    fillRegion.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fillRegion.ClipsDescendants = true
    fillRegion.ZIndex = 2
    Instance.new("UICorner", fillRegion).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    
    local fillRegGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", fillRegion)
    fillRegGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 238), Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 230))
    fillRegGradient.Rotation = 90
    
    local fillRegStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", fillRegion)
    fillRegStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
    fillRegStroke.Thickness = 1
    fillRegStroke.Transparency = 0.72
    
    local progressFill = Instance.new("Frame", fillRegion)
    progressFill.Name = "Fill"
    progressFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    progressFill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    progressFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 215) -- heller Füllbalken
    progressFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    progressFill.ZIndex = 3
    Instance.new("UICorner", progressFill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    
    local fillGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", progressFill)
    fillGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 235), Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 210))
    fillGradient.Rotation = 90
    
    local stealLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", fillRegion)
    stealLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 1, 0)
    stealLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    stealLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    stealLbl.Text = "AUTO GRAB"
    stealLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)  -- dunkler für Kontrast
    stealLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    stealLbl.TextSize = 11
    stealLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    stealLbl.ZIndex = 5
    
    local progressPct = Instance.new("TextLabel", fillRegion)
    progressPct.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 1, 0)
    progressPct.Position = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 0)
    progressPct.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    progressPct.Text = "0%"
    progressPct.TextColor3 = THEME_ACCENT_BRIGHT
    progressPct.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
    progressPct.TextSize = 12
    progressPct.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
    progressPct.ZIndex = 5

    local perfLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", fillRegion)
    perfLabel.Name = "Perf"
    perfLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 16)
    perfLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -75, 0, 1)
    perfLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    perfLabel.Text = "60 FPS | -- ms"
    perfLabel.TextColor3 = THEME_ACCENT_BRIGHT
    perfLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    perfLabel.TextSize = 15
    perfLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 248)
    perfLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = ViperOverheadStrokeColor()
    perfLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.12
    perfLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    perfLabel.ZIndex = 6

    local barState = "IDLE"
    function StealBarSetTheme(color)
        if not color then return end

        THEME_ACCENT = color
        THEME_ACCENT_BRIGHT = color:Lerp(Color3.new(1,1,1), 0.25)
        THEME_ACCENT_DIM = color:Lerp(Color3.new(1,1,1), 0.78)

        -- The original bar is white. Make that OUTER shell/rim the selected color.
        pbFrame.BackgroundColor3 = color:Lerp(Color3.new(1,1,1), 0.72)
        pbSt.Color = color
        pbSt.Thickness = 2
        pbSt.Transparency = 0

        -- Keep the inside light like the original design.
        fillRegion.BackgroundColor3 = color:Lerp(Color3.new(1,1,1), 0.88)
        fillRegStroke.Color = color
        fillRegStroke.Transparency = 0.20

        progressFill.BackgroundColor3 = color
        fillGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new(
            color:Lerp(Color3.new(1,1,1), 0.18),
            color:Lerp(Color3.new(0,0,0), 0.12)
        )

        -- All Steal Bar text follows the selected color.
        stealLbl.TextColor3 = color
        progressPct.TextColor3 = color
        perfLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 248)
        perfLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = (viperTextColor == "White") and Color3.fromRGB(22,24,30) or color
        perfLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.12
    end
    function setBarState(state)
        barState = state
        if state == "STEALING" then
            TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = THEME_ACCENT_BRIGHT}):Play()
            TS:Create(fillRegion, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = THEME_ACCENT_DIM}):Play()
        elseif state == "READY" then
            TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = THEME_ACCENT_BRIGHT}):Play()
            TS:Create(fillRegion, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = THEME_ACCENT_DIM}):Play()
            progressPct.Text = "0%"
            progressPct.TextColor3 = THEME_ACCENT_BRIGHT
        else
            TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = THEME_ACCENT}):Play()
            TS:Create(fillRegion, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = THEME_ACCENT_DIM}):Play()
            progressPct.Text = "0%"
            progressPct.TextColor3 = THEME_ACCENT
        end
    end
    
    local StealBar = {}
    function StealBar.SetProgress(p)
        p = math.clamp(p, 0, 1)
        progressFill.Size = UDim2.new(p, 0, 1, 0)
        progressPct.Text = math.floor(p * 100 + 0.5) .. "%"
    end
    function StealBar.Reset()
        StealBar.SetProgress(0)
        setBarState("IDLE")
    end
    function StealBar.SetState(state)
        setBarState(state)
    end
    setBarState("IDLE")
    task.spawn(function()
        local frames, last = 0, os.clock()
        RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function() frames = frames + 1 end)
        while gui.Parent do
            task.wait(0.5)
            local now=os.clock()
            local fps=math.floor(frames/math.max(now-last,0.01)+0.5)
            frames=0; last=now
            local ping="--"
            pcall(function() ping=tostring(math.floor(Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()+0.5)) end)
            perfLabel.Text=string.format("%d FPS | %s ms",fps,ping)
        end
    end)
    function StealBar.SetTheme(color) StealBarSetTheme(color) end
    StealBarSetTheme(ViperTextColorPalette[viperTextColor] or ViperTextColorPalette.White)
    _G.StealBar = StealBar
end
_G.__ViperDuelsSetupStealBar()
if _G.StealBar and _G.StealBar.SetTheme then _G.StealBar.SetTheme(ViperTextColorPalette[viperTextColor] or ViperTextColorPalette.White) end

-- ============================================================
-- CLEAN PLAYER OVERHEAD HUD: Discord + live Speed
-- ============================================================
_G.__ViperDuelsSetupOverheadHud = function()
    local character = LP.Character or LP.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or character:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
    if not head then return end

    -- Remove every previous Viper overhead variant so exactly ONE billboard remains.
    for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("BillboardGui") and (
            child.Name == "ViperOverheadHUD"
            or child.Name == "ViperDuelsOverheadInfo"
            or child.Name == "ViperDuelsSpeed"
            or child.Name == "ViperHubOverhead"
        ) then
            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
        end
    end

    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "ViperOverheadHUD"
    billboard.Adornee = head
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.LightInfluence = 0
    billboard.MaxDistance = 250
    billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 270, 0, 100)
    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.55, 0)
    billboard.ResetOnSpawn = false
    billboard.Parent = head

    local discord = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    discord.Name = "Discord"
    discord.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    discord.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 31)
    discord.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    discord.Text = "discord.gg/Viperhub"
    local themeColor = ViperTextColorPalette[viperTextColor] or ViperTextColorPalette.White
    local crispStroke = Color3.fromRGB(8, 10, 15)
    discord.TextColor3 = themeColor
    discord.TextStrokeColor3 = crispStroke
    discord.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18
    discord.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    discord.TextSize = 20
    discord.TextScaled = false
    discord.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    discord.ZIndex = 3
    discord.Parent = billboard

    local speedBox = Instance.new("Frame")
    speedBox.Name = "SpeedBox"
    speedBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 132, 0, 45)
    speedBox.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -66, 0, 34)
    speedBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 14, 22)
    speedBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    speedBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    speedBox.ZIndex = 2
    speedBox.Parent = billboard

    local speedCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    speedCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
    speedCorner.Parent = speedBox

    local speedStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    speedStroke.Color = themeColor
    speedStroke.Thickness = 2.0
    speedStroke.Transparency = 0.02
    speedStroke.Parent = speedBox

    local speedTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    speedTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    speedTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
    speedTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 3)
    speedTitle.Text = "SPEED"
    speedTitle.TextColor3 = themeColor
    speedTitle.TextStrokeColor3 = crispStroke
    speedTitle.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18
    speedTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    speedTitle.TextSize = 10
    speedTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    speedTitle.ZIndex = 4
    speedTitle.Parent = speedBox

    local speedValue = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    speedValue.Name = "Value"
    speedValue.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    speedValue.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 24)
    speedValue.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 16)
    speedValue.Text = "0.0  stud/s"
    speedValue.TextColor3 = themeColor
    speedValue.TextStrokeColor3 = crispStroke
    speedValue.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18
    speedValue.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    speedValue.TextSize = 14
    speedValue.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    speedValue.ZIndex = 4
    speedValue.Parent = speedBox

    -- Optional ragdoll countdown is inside this SAME billboard.
    ragdollCountdownLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    ragdollCountdownLabel.Name = "RagdollCountdown"
    ragdollCountdownLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ragdollCountdownLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
    ragdollCountdownLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 80)
    ragdollCountdownLabel.Text = ""
    ragdollCountdownLabel.Visible = false
    ragdollCountdownLabel.TextColor3 = themeColor
    ragdollCountdownLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = crispStroke
    ragdollCountdownLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.18
    ragdollCountdownLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    ragdollCountdownLabel.TextSize = 13
    ragdollCountdownLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    ragdollCountdownLabel.ZIndex = 5
    ragdollCountdownLabel.Parent = billboard

    overheadGui = billboard
    overheadTitleLabel = discord
    overheadSpeedLabel = speedValue
    overheadBoxStroke = speedStroke

    local function updateOverhead()
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char or not billboard.Parent then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local velocity = root and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
        -- Show the player's REAL current movement speed, not the configured WalkSpeed.
        -- Horizontal velocity is used so jumping/falling does not inflate the value.
        local speed = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z).Magnitude
        speedValue.Text = string.format("%.1f  stud/s", speed)
    end

    pcall(applyOverheadTheme)

    task.spawn(function()
        while billboard.Parent do
            updateOverhead()
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end)
end
_G.__ViperDuelsSetupOverheadHud()
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.75)
    _G.__ViperDuelsSetupOverheadHud()
    pcall(applyOverheadTheme)
    if ragdollCountdownEnabled then hookRagdollCountdown(char) end
end)
if ragdollCountdownEnabled and LP.Character then hookRagdollCountdown(LP.Character) end
if _G.ViperAutoStealSync then task.defer(_G.ViperAutoStealSync) end

_G.__ViperDuelsMinimized = false
_G.__ViperDuelsClosed = false

-- X = real close. It hides both windows; Toggle UI / MiniButton can reopen it.
Close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
_G.__ViperDuelsClosed = true
_G.__ViperDuelsMinimized = false
Main.Visible = false
MiniFrame.Visible = false
pcall(saveViperConfig)
end)

Minimize.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
_G.__ViperDuelsClosed = false
_G.__ViperDuelsMinimized = true
Main.Visible = false
MiniFrame.Visible = true
pcall(saveViperConfig)
end)
end
_G.__ViperDuelsSetupMinimizeToggle()

-- FINAL SETTINGS RESTORE: run after every tab/control/function exists.
task.defer(function()
    task.wait(0.15)
    pcall(function() if _G.ViperDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates then _G.ViperDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates() end end)
    pcall(function() if ViperApplyAvatarVisuals then ViperApplyAvatarVisuals(LP.Character) end end)
    pcall(function() if setAutoResetOnDeathVisual then setAutoResetOnDeathVisual(autoResetOnDeathEnabled == true) end end)
end)

--// VIPER DEVICE PROFILE
-- Mobile is a comfort/responsive profile, NOT a reduced-settings mode.
-- Every existing tab/control remains available; only sizing/spacing/input comfort changes.
_G.__ViperDeviceRefs = _G.__ViperDeviceRefs or {}
do
    local r = _G.__ViperDeviceRefs
    r.PlayerGui, r.UserInputService = PlayerGui, UserInputService
    r.Main, r.Sidebar, r.Content = Main, Sidebar, Content
    r.Title, r.Discord = Title, Discord
    r.ContentBackdrop = ContentBackdrop
    r.FULL_MAIN_SIZE = FULL_MAIN_SIZE
    r.corner, r.stroke, r.COLORS = corner, stroke, COLORS
end

_G.ViperDetectDevice = function()
    local r = _G.__ViperDeviceRefs
    return (r.UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not r.UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled) and "Mobile" or "PC"
end

_G.ViperApplyDeviceProfile = function(mode)
    local r = _G.__ViperDeviceRefs
    mode = (mode == "Mobile" or mode == "PC") and mode or _G.ViperDetectDevice()
    _G.ViperDeviceMode = mode
    _G.ViperIsMobile = mode == "Mobile"
    _G.ViperMobileProfileApplied = _G.ViperIsMobile

    -- IMPORTANT: device selection must NOT resize the main GUI.
    -- GUI Scale and Mobile Button Size are separate settings.
    -- Keep the normal Main/Sidebar/Content layout untouched on both devices.

    local mg = r.PlayerGui and r.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ViperMobileButtons")
    if mg then
        mg.Enabled = (_G.ViperDeviceMode == "Mobile")
            and not (_G.ViperHideMobileButtons == true)
            and (Main.Visible == true)
    end

    -- Device selection owns the mobile-button visibility:
    -- Mobile => ON, PC => OFF. This intentionally overrides an old
    -- hide/show preference when the user explicitly chooses a device.
    if mode == "Mobile" then
        _G.ViperHideMobileButtons = false
    else
        _G.ViperHideMobileButtons = true
    end

    if _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonsHidden then
        pcall(_G.ViperApplyMobileButtonsHidden)
    end

    if mode == "Mobile" and _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonSize then
        pcall(_G.ViperApplyMobileButtonSize)
    end

    if setHideMobileButtonsVisual then
        pcall(setHideMobileButtonsVisual, _G.ViperHideMobileButtons == true)
    end

    if saveViperConfig then
        pcall(saveViperConfig)
    end
end

local VIPER_DEVICE_PICKER_ONCE_FILE = "ViperDuels_DevicePicker_Once.flag"
local function viperShouldShowOneTimeDevicePicker()
    if not (isfile and writefile) then
        return false
    end
    local exists = false
    pcall(function() exists = isfile(VIPER_DEVICE_PICKER_ONCE_FILE) end)
    return not exists
end
local function viperMarkDevicePickerUsed()
    if not writefile then return end
    pcall(function()
        writefile(VIPER_DEVICE_PICKER_ONCE_FILE, "used")
    end)
end

_G.ViperShowDevicePicker = function(done)
    local r = _G.__ViperDeviceRefs
    local pg = r.PlayerGui
    local old = pg:FindFirstChild("ViperDevicePicker")
    if old then old:Destroy() end

    local g = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    g.Name = "ViperDevicePicker"
    g.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    g.ResetOnSpawn = false
    g.DisplayOrder = 1000
    g.Parent = pg

    local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
    bg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
    bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(7,7,9)
    bg.BackgroundTransparency = .12
    bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bg.Parent = g

    -- Small, centered premium picker.
    local card = Instance.new("Frame")
    card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
    card.Position = UDim2.fromScale(.5,.5)
    card.Size = UDim2.new(0,360,0,218)
    card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16,16,20)
    card.BorderSizePixel = 0
    card.Parent = bg
    r.corner(card,16)
    r.stroke(card,r.COLORS.stroke,1.2,.18)

    local accent = Instance.new("Frame")
    accent.Size = UDim2.new(0,4,1,-28)
    accent.Position = UDim2.new(0,12,0,14)
    accent.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220,220,225)
    accent.BorderSizePixel = 0
    accent.Parent = card
    r.corner(accent,2)

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Position = UDim2.new(0,30,0,20)
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1,-60,0,30)
    title.Text = "VIPER DUELS ON TOP"
    title.TextColor3 = r.COLORS.white
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    title.TextSize = 21
    title.Parent = card

    local sub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    sub.Position = UDim2.new(0,30,0,51)
    sub.Size = UDim2.new(1,-60,0,22)
    sub.Text = "Choose your device • change it later in Settings"
    sub.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150,150,160)
    sub.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    sub.TextSize = 11
    sub.Parent = card

    local function addChoice(labelText,x,desc)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Size = UDim2.new(0,148,0,78)
        b.Position = UDim2.new(0,x,0,91)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(23,23,28)
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Text = ""
        b.AutoButtonColor = false
        b.Parent = card
        r.corner(b,13)
        r.stroke(b,r.COLORS.strokeSoft,1,.28)

        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0,9)
        t.Size = UDim2.new(1,-20,0,24)
        t.Text = labelText
        t.TextColor3 = r.COLORS.white
        t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        t.TextSize = 16
        t.Parent = b

        local d = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        d.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        d.Position = UDim2.new(0,9,0,36)
        d.Size = UDim2.new(1,-18,0,28)
        d.Text = desc
        d.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(145,145,155)
        d.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        d.TextSize = 9
        d.TextWrapped = true
        d.Parent = b

        b.Activated:Connect(function()
            _G.ViperApplyDeviceProfile(labelText == "MOBILE" and "Mobile" or "PC")
            viperMarkDevicePickerUsed()
            g:Destroy()
            if done then task.defer(done) end
        end)
    end

    addChoice("PC",30,"Full desktop layout")
    addChoice("MOBILE",182,"Touch-friendly layout")
end

_G.__ViperDuelsRunIntro = function()
local TS = TweenService
local introGuiParent = Gui and Gui.Parent or PlayerGui
local origSize = getViperUIStyleSize() or Main.Size
local wasMinimizedBeforeIntro = (_G.__ViperDuelsMinimized == true)
if not _introEnabled then
stopIntroPlayback()
stopIntroPreview()
Main.Size = origSize
if not wasMinimizedBeforeIntro then
Main.Visible = true
local mg = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ViperMobileButtons")
if mg then mg.Enabled = (_G.ViperDeviceMode == "Mobile") and not (_G.ViperHideMobileButtons == true) end
MiniFrame.Visible = false
else
Main.Visible = false
MiniFrame.Visible = true
end
return
end
playIntroMusic()
Main.Visible = false
MiniFrame.Visible = false
Main.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
task.spawn(function()
local introGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
introGui.Name = "ViperDuelsIntro"
introGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
introGui.DisplayOrder = 100
introGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
introGui.Parent = introGuiParent
local introActive = true
function finishIntro()
if not introActive then return end
introActive = false
stopIntroPlayback()
MiniFrame.Visible = false
Main.Visible = true
pcall(function()
local mg = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ViperMobileButtons")
if mg then mg.Enabled = (_G.ViperDeviceMode == "Mobile") and not (_G.ViperHideMobileButtons == true) end
end)
pcall(function()
TS:Create(Main, TweenInfo.new(0.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = origSize}):Play()
end)
task.delay(0.35, function()
pcall(function() introGui:Destroy() end)
end)
end
local darkBg = Instance.new("Frame", introGui)
darkBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)

darkBg.ZIndex = 1

bgGrad.Rotation = 90
local redWash = Instance.new("Frame", introGui)
redWash.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
redWash.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
redWash.BackgroundTransparency = 1
redWash.BorderSizePixel = 0
redWash.ZIndex = 2
local skipBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", introGui)
skipBtn.Name = "SkipIntro"
skipBtn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0)
skipBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1,-22,0,22)
skipBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0,104,0,34)
skipBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24,24,27)
skipBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
skipBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
