task.delay(3.8, function()
if introActive then
finishIntro()
end
end)
function makeViperCard(parent, size, z)
local chip = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
chip.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
chip.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
chip.BorderSizePixel = 0
chip.BackgroundTransparency = 1
chip.ZIndex = z or 6
Instance.new("UICorner", chip).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

chip.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 21)

local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", chip)
stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(196, 166, 92)
stroke.Thickness = math.max(1, math.floor(size * 0.025))
stroke.Transparency = 1
stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

local inner = Instance.new("Frame", chip)
inner.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
inner.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
inner.Size = UDim2.new(0.58, 0, 0.58, 0)
inner.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 31)
inner.BackgroundTransparency = 1
inner.BorderSizePixel = 0
inner.ZIndex = (z or 6) + 1
Instance.new("UICorner", inner).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

local valueText = Instance.new("TextLabel", inner)
valueText.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
valueText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
valueText.Text = "V"
valueText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(214, 202, 168)
valueText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
valueText.TextScaled = true
valueText.TextTransparency = 1
valueText.ZIndex = (z or 6) + 2

for i = 1, 2 do
local mark = Instance.new("Frame", chip)
mark.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
mark.Size = UDim2.new(0.18, 0, 0.045, 0)
mark.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(196, 166, 92)
mark.BackgroundTransparency = 1
mark.BorderSizePixel = 0
mark.Rotation = (i - 1) * 90
mark.ZIndex = (z or 6) + 1
local angle = math.rad((i - 1) * 90)
local radius = size * 0.39
mark.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.cos(angle) * radius, 0.5, math.sin(angle) * radius)
Instance.new("UICorner", mark).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
end

return chip, {valueText}, stroke
end
local cards = {}
for i = 1, 6 do
local size = math.random(40, 60)
local card, labels, stroke = makeViperCard(introGui, size, 5 + i)
local side = (i % 2 == 0) and -0.35 or 1.35
local targetSide = (i % 2 == 0) and 1.35 or -0.35
local y = math.random(4, 96) / 100
card.Position = UDim2.new(side, 0, y, 0)
card.Rotation = math.random(-40, 40)
cards[i] = {frame=card, labels=labels, stroke=stroke, startX=side, endX=targetSide, y=y, speed=0.09+math.random()*0.10, bob=math.random()*6.28, rot=math.random(-55,55), drift=math.random(-14,14)/100}
end

local function makeGoldLine(position, size, rotation)
local line = Instance.new("Frame", introGui)

line.Position = position
line.Size = size
line.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(126, 108, 68)
line.BackgroundTransparency = 1
line.BorderSizePixel = 0
line.Rotation = rotation or 0
line.ZIndex = 17
Instance.new("UICorner", line).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
table.insert(mafiaProps, line)
return line
end

makeGoldLine(UDim2.new(0.18,0,0.28,0), UDim2.new(0,72,0,2), -18)

}

local titleShadow = Instance.new("TextLabel", center)

TS:Create(darkBg, TweenInfo.new(0.65), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.22}):Play()

TS:Create(cd.frame, TweenInfo.new(0.65), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.08}):Play()

end
end
end)

BackgroundTransparency = 0.38
}):Play()

if not introActive then pcall(function() driftConn:Disconnect() end); return end
TS:Create(center, TweenInfo.new(0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)}):Play()

TS:Create(lineBot, TweenInfo.new(0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {Size = UDim2.new(0,0,0,2)}):Play()
TS:Create(viperLogo, TweenInfo.new(0.55, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,1.25,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Rotation = 28}):Play()
for _, lbl in ipairs(viperLabels) do TS:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.45), {TextTransparency = 1}):Play() end
if viperStroke then TS:Create(viperStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.45), {Transparency = 1}):Play() end
for _, prop in ipairs(mafiaProps) do
if prop and prop.Parent then
TS:Create(prop, TweenInfo.new(0.22), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
end
end
TS:Create(darkBg, TweenInfo.new(0.75), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
for _, cd in ipairs(cards) do
TS:Create(cd.frame, TweenInfo.new(0.55), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
if cd.stroke then TS:Create(cd.stroke, TweenInfo.new(0.55), {Transparency = 1}):Play() end
for _, lbl in ipairs(cd.labels) do TS:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.55), {TextTransparency = 1}):Play() end
end
Main.Visible = true
MiniFrame.Visible = false
TS:Create(Main, TweenInfo.new(0.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = origSize}):Play()
task.wait(0.9)
introActive = false
pcall(function() driftConn:Disconnect() end)
pcall(function() introGui:Destroy() end)
end)
end
-- Device picker: show one final time if its persistent one-time flag is missing.
-- After the user chooses PC/MOBILE, the flag is written and future executions skip it.
local _viperForceOneTimePicker = viperShouldShowOneTimeDevicePicker()
if _viperForceOneTimePicker or (_G.ViperDeviceMode ~= "PC" and _G.ViperDeviceMode ~= "Mobile") then
    Main.Visible = false
    MiniFrame.Visible = false
    task.defer(function()
        local mg = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ViperMobileButtons")
        if mg then mg.Enabled = false end
    end)
    _G.ViperShowDevicePicker(function()
        Main.Visible = false
        MiniFrame.Visible = false
        _G.__ViperDuelsRunIntro()
    end)
else
    _G.ViperApplyDeviceProfile(_G.ViperDeviceMode)
    Main.Visible = false
    MiniFrame.Visible = false
    task.defer(function()
        local mg = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ViperMobileButtons")
        if mg then mg.Enabled = false end
    end)
    _G.__ViperDuelsRunIntro()
end

_G.ViperDuelsForceSyncLoadedButtons = function()
pcall(function()
if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(autoTPEnabled) end
if autoTPEnabled then startAutoTP() else stopAutoTP() end
end)
pcall(function()
if setInfJumpVisual then setInfJumpVisual(InfiniteJump.Enabled) end
if setInfJumpInternal then setInfJumpInternal(InfiniteJump.Enabled) end
end)
pcall(function()
if setAntiRagdollVisual then setAntiRagdollVisual(antiRagdollEnabled) end
setAntiRagdoll(antiRagdollEnabled)
end)
pcall(function()
if _G.ViperSetAutoLeft then _G.ViperSetAutoLeft(autoLeftEnabled, true) end
if _G.ViperSetAutoRight then _G.ViperSetAutoRight(autoRightEnabled, true) end
end)
pcall(function()
if setAutoStealVisual then setAutoStealVisual(autoStealEnabled) end
if _G.ViperAutoStealSync then _G.ViperAutoStealSync() end
end)
pcall(function()
if _G.ViperNormalAutoSwingSetVisual then _G.ViperNormalAutoSwingSetVisual(autoSwingEnabled) end
if _G.ViperMirrorTPDownSetVisual then _G.ViperMirrorTPDownSetVisual(mirrorTPDownEnabled) end
if _G.ViperAntiDesyncAutoSwingSetVisual then _G.ViperAntiDesyncAutoSwingSetVisual(antiDesyncAutoSwingEnabled) end
if _G.ViperAntiDesyncSetVisual then _G.ViperAntiDesyncSetVisual(_G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true) end
if _G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn and _G.ViperStartAntiDesyncAimbot then
_G.ViperStartAntiDesyncAimbot()
elseif _G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot then
_G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot()
end
end)
pcall(function()
if selectedAimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" then
if _G.ViperNormalAimbotStop then _G.ViperNormalAimbotStop() end
if _G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn and _G.ViperAntiBypassStart then
_G.ViperAntiBypassStart()
elseif _G.ViperAntiBypassStop then
_G.ViperAntiBypassStop()
end
else
if _G.ViperAntiBypassStop then _G.ViperAntiBypassStop() end
if _G.ViperNormalAimbotOn and _G.ViperNormalAimbotStart then
_G.ViperNormalAimbotStart()
elseif _G.ViperNormalAimbotStop then
_G.ViperNormalAimbotStop()
end
end
if _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual() end
end)
pcall(function()
if setBatCounterVisual then setBatCounterVisual(batCounterEnabled) end
if setMedCounterVisual then setMedCounterVisual(medCounterEnabled) end
if setSafeModeVisual then setSafeModeVisual(antiKickEnabled) end
if batCounterEnabled then
if _G.ViperStartBatCounter then _G.ViperStartBatCounter() end
else
if _G.ViperStopBatCounter then _G.ViperStopBatCounter() end
end
if medCounterEnabled then
if _G.ViperStartMedCounter then _G.ViperStartMedCounter(LP.Character) end
else
if _G.ViperStopMedCounter then _G.ViperStopMedCounter() end
end
if _G.ViperSetNoPlayerCollisionVisual then _G.ViperSetNoPlayerCollisionVisual(_G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionEnabled) end
if _G.ViperNoPlayerCollisionEnabled then
if enableNoPlayerCollision then enableNoPlayerCollision() end
else
if disableNoPlayerCollision then disableNoPlayerCollision() end
end
if _G.ViperSetAutoResetOnMed then
_G.ViperSetAutoResetOnMed(autoResetOnMedEnabled, true)
else
if setAutoResetOnMedVisual then setAutoResetOnMedVisual(autoResetOnMedEnabled) end
end
end)

if setPlayerESPVisual then setPlayerESPVisual(espEnabled) end
if espEnabled then if startPlayerESP then startPlayerESP() end; if BoxedESPOptions then BoxedESPOptions.box = true end else if stopPlayerESP then stopPlayerESP() end; if BoxedESPOptions then BoxedESPOptions.box = false end end
if setTracerESPVisual then setTracerESPVisual(showTracerEnabled) end
if BoxedESPOptions then BoxedESPOptions.tracer = showTracerEnabled end
if refreshBoxedESP then refreshBoxedESP() end
if setRagdollCountdownVisual then setRagdollCountdownVisual(ragdollCountdownEnabled) end
if ragdollCountdownEnabled then hookRagdollCountdown(LP.Character) else stopRagdollCountdown() end
if setFPSBoostVisual then setFPSBoostVisual(fpsBoostEnabled) end
if fpsBoostEnabled then enableStretchRez() else disableStretchRez() end
if antiLagEnabled then enableAntiLag() else disableAntiLag() end
if setAntiLagVisual then setAntiLagVisual(antiLagEnabled == true) end
if viperAntiDieEnabled then startViperAntiDie() else stopViperAntiDie() end
if setViperAntiDieVisual then setViperAntiDieVisual(viperAntiDieEnabled == true) end
if setNukeOptimiserVisual then setNukeOptimiserVisual(nukeOptimiserEnabled) end
if nukeOptimiserEnabled then enableNukeOptimizer() else disableNukeOptimizer() end
pcall(function() applyGraphicsProfile(graphicsMode) end)
if setFOVVisual then setFOVVisual(fovEnabled) end
if fovEnabled then enableCustomFov() else disableCustomFov() end
if setNoCamCollisionVisual then setNoCamCollisionVisual(noCamCollisionEnabled) end
if noCamCollisionEnabled then enableNoCamCollision() else disableNoCamCollision() end
if type(applyCustomSky) == "function" then
applyCustomSky((skyTheme and skyTheme ~= "") and skyTheme or "Off")
end
if skyValueLabel then skyValueLabel.Text = skyTheme or "Off" end
end)
pcall(saveViperConfig)
end

task.wait(0.35)
if type(applyCustomSky) == "function" then
pcall(function() applyCustomSky((skyTheme and skyTheme ~= "") and skyTheme or "Off") end)
end
if skyValueLabel then skyValueLabel.Text = skyTheme or "Off" end

_G.ViperAutoTPRestoreWanted = _G.ViperAutoTPRestoreWanted or false
_G.ViperAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = _G.ViperAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil or 0
function viperAnyAimbotActive()
return (_G.ViperNormalAimbotOn == true) or (_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn == true) or (_G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true) or (_G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true)
end
_G.ViperStopAutoTPForAction = function()
if autoTPEnabled then
_G.ViperAutoTPRestoreWanted = true
_G.ViperAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.35
stopAutoTP()
if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(false) end
end
end
function viperTryRestoreAutoTP()
if not _G.ViperAutoTPRestoreWanted then return end
if tick() < (_G.ViperAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil or 0) then return end
if viperAnyAimbotActive() then return end
if dropBrainrotActive then return end
_G.ViperAutoTPRestoreWanted = false
startAutoTP()
if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(true) end
saveViperConfig()
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(viperTryRestoreAutoTP)
_G._oldViperStopNormalAimbot = _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot
_G.ViperStopNormalAimbot = function(...)
local r = {_G._oldViperStopNormalAimbot(...)}
_G.ViperAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
task.delay(0.08, viperTryRestoreAutoTP)
return unpack(r)
end
_G.ViperNormalAimbotStop = _G.ViperStopNormalAimbot
_G._oldViperStopAntiBypassAimbot = _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot
_G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot = function(...)
local r = {_G._oldViperStopAntiBypassAimbot(...)}
_G.ViperAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
task.delay(0.08, viperTryRestoreAutoTP)
return unpack(r)
end
_G.ViperAntiBypassStop = _G.ViperStopAntiBypassAimbot
_G._oldViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot = _G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot
_G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot = function(...)
local r = {_G._oldViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot(...)}
_G.ViperAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
task.delay(0.08, viperTryRestoreAutoTP)
return unpack(r)
end
task.spawn(function()
local wasDropping = false
while task.wait(0.05) do
if dropBrainrotActive then
wasDropping = true
elseif wasDropping then
wasDropping = false
_G.ViperAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
task.delay(0.08, viperTryRestoreAutoTP)
end
end
end)
function viperRepairKeybinds()
for keyId, defaultKey in pairs(DEFAULT_SPEED_KEYBINDS) do
if speedKeybinds[keyId] == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
speedKeybinds[keyId] = defaultKey
end
end
if tpDownKeybind == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then tpDownKeybind = DEFAULT_TP_DOWN_KEYBIND end
if refreshAllSpeedKeybinds then refreshAllSpeedKeybinds() end
if refreshTPDownKeybind then refreshTPDownKeybind() end
end
_G._oldSaveViperConfigStable = saveViperConfig
saveViperConfig = function()
viperRepairKeybinds()
return _G._oldSaveViperConfigStable()
end
viperRepairKeybinds()
task.defer(function()
task.wait(0.2)
viperRepairKeybinds()
saveViperConfig()
end)
task.defer(function()
task.wait(0.35)
local TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
local old = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ViperMobileButtons")
if old then old:Destroy() end
local mobileGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
mobileGui.Name = "ViperMobileButtons"
mobileGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
mobileGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
mobileGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
mobileGui.DisplayOrder = 1000
mobileGui.Enabled = Main.Visible and _G.ViperDeviceMode == "Mobile"
mobileGui.Parent = PlayerGui
_G.ViperMobileButtonRefs = {}
local mobileButtons = _G.ViperMobileButtonRefs
function _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonsHidden()
local g = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ViperMobileButtons")
if g then g.Enabled = (_G.ViperDeviceMode == "Mobile") and not (_G.ViperHideMobileButtons == true) end
if setHideMobileButtonsVisual then pcall(setHideMobileButtonsVisual, _G.ViperHideMobileButtons == true) end
end
function _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonColor()
    local palette={White=Color3.fromRGB(235,235,240),Blue=Color3.fromRGB(70,140,255),Cyan=Color3.fromRGB(0,220,255),Purple=Color3.fromRGB(170,90,255),Pink=Color3.fromRGB(255,80,170),Red=Color3.fromRGB(255,80,80),Green=Color3.fromRGB(80,220,120),Orange=Color3.fromRGB(255,150,60)}
    local c=palette[_G.ViperMobileButtonColor or "White"] or palette.White
    for _,entry in pairs(mobileButtons) do
        local btn=entry and entry.btn
        if btn then btn.BackgroundColor3=c end
    end
end
function _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonShape()
    local shape = _G.ViperMobileButtonShape or "Circle"
    for _, entry in pairs(mobileButtons) do
        local btn = entry and entry.btn
        if btn then
            local c = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
            if c then
                if shape == "Square" then c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
                elseif shape == "Rounded" then c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
                else c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) end
            end
        end
    end
end
function _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonSize()
_G.ViperMobileButtonScale = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.ViperMobileButtonScale) or 0.75, 0.30, 1.35)
for _, entry in pairs(mobileButtons) do
local holder = entry and entry.holder
if holder then
local sc = holder:FindFirstChild("MobileButtonScale") or Instance.new("UIScale")
sc.Name = "MobileButtonScale"
sc.Scale = _G.ViperMobileButtonScale
sc.Parent = holder
end
end
pcall(function()
local gui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ViperDuelsAdaptReconstruct") or PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("AdaptHubPolished") or PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("CyberHub")
local root = gui or PlayerGui
for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
if obj.Name == "Mobile Buttons Size" then
local valueBox = obj:FindFirstChild("Value")
if valueBox and valueBox:IsA("TextLabel") then
valueBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", _G.ViperMobileButtonScale)
end
end
end
end)
end
local function setActive(btn, state)
if not btn then return end
local pressed = btn:GetAttribute("ViperMobilePressed") == true
state = (state == true) or pressed
local visualState = state and "on" or "off"
if btn:GetAttribute("ViperMobileVisualState") == visualState then return end
btn:SetAttribute("ViperMobileVisualState", visualState)
local holder = btn.Parent
local glow = holder and holder:FindFirstChild("Glow")
local st = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
BackgroundColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(56,56,62) or Color3.fromRGB(16,16,19),
TextColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255) or Color3.fromRGB(214,214,224),
TextTransparency = 0,
}):Play()
if st then
TS:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
Color = state and Color3.fromRGB(176,176,184) or Color3.fromRGB(62,62,70),
Thickness = 1,
Transparency = state and 0 or 0.4,
}):Play()
end
if glow then
glow.Visible = false
glow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
local gs = glow:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
if gs then gs.Transparency = 1 end
end
end
local function pulse(btn)
if not btn then return end
btn:SetAttribute("ViperMobilePressed", true)
setActive(btn, true)
task.delay(0.18, function()
if btn and btn.Parent then
btn:SetAttribute("ViperMobilePressed", false)
setActive(btn, false)
end
end)
end
local function makeButton(key, label, pos, onPress)
local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
holder.Name = "MBH_" .. key
holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 64)
holder.Position = tableToUDim2(_G.ViperMobileButtonPositions[key], pos)
holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
holder.ZIndex = 1000
holder.Active = true
holder.Parent = mobileGui
local glow = Instance.new("Frame", holder)
glow.Name = "Glow"
glow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 6, 1, 6)
glow.Position = UDim2.new(0, -3, 0, -3)
glow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
glow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
glow.BorderSizePixel = 0
glow.ZIndex = 1000
Instance.new("UICorner", glow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
local glowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", glow)
glowStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
glowStroke.Thickness = 0.8
glowStroke.Transparency = 1
glowStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", holder)
btn.Name = "MB_" .. key
btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18,18,24)
btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
btn.Text = label
btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(214,214,224)
btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
btn.TextSize = 9
btn.TextWrapped = true
btn.AutoButtonColor = false
btn.ZIndex = 1002
btn.Active = true
local btnCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", btn)
if _G.ViperMobileButtonShape == "Square" then
    btnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 0)
elseif _G.ViperMobileButtonShape == "Rounded" then
    btnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
else
    btnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
end
local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(74,74,90)
stroke.Thickness = 1
stroke.Transparency = 0.58
stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
local pressing, dragging = false, false
local pressPos, holderStart = nil, nil
btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
pressing = true
dragging = false
pressPos = i.Position
holderStart = holder.Position
btn:SetAttribute("ViperMobilePressed", true)
setActive(btn, true)
pcall(function()
TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.045, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2),
Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, -4)
}):Play()
end)
end
end)
btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
if dragging then saveViperConfig() end
pressing = false
dragging = false
btn:SetAttribute("ViperMobilePressed", false)
pcall(function()
TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.055, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
}):Play()
end)
task.delay(0.08, function()
if btn and btn.Parent then
local keepOn = false
if key == "autoLeft" then keepOn = autoLeftEnabled == true
elseif key == "autoRight" then keepOn = autoRightEnabled == true
elseif key == "aimbot" then keepOn = (_G.ViperNormalAimbotOn == true) or (_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn == true)
elseif key == "antiDesync" then keepOn = _G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true
elseif key == "carry" then keepOn = currentSpeedMode == "Carry"
elseif key == "laggerNormal" then keepOn = currentSpeedMode == "Lagger"
elseif key == "laggerCarry" then keepOn = currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry"
end
setActive(btn, keepOn)
end
end)
end
end)
btn.Activated:Connect(function()
    if not dragging then
        pcall(onPress, btn)
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
if _G.ViperGuiLocked == true or not pressing then return end
if i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
local delta = i.Position - pressPos
if not dragging and (math.abs(delta.X) > 6 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 6) then dragging = true end
if dragging then
holder.Position = UDim2.new(holderStart.X.Scale, holderStart.X.Offset + delta.X, holderStart.Y.Scale, holderStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
end
end)
mobileButtons[key] = {holder = holder, btn = btn, setActive = function(state) setActive(btn, state) end}
return btn
end
-- Mobile layout: use the bottom-right safe area instead of a fixed
-- center offset. This behaves better across phone aspect ratios.
local function mobileViewportDefaults()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local vp = cam and cam.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(390, 844)
    local right = math.max(10, math.floor(vp.X * 0.025))
    local bottom = math.max(18, math.floor(vp.Y * 0.025))
    local gap = 8
    local size = 64
    local x3 = -(right + size)
    local x2 = x3 - gap - size
    local x1 = x2 - gap - size
    local y4 = -(bottom + size)
    local y3 = y4 - gap - size
    local y2 = y3 - gap - size
    local y1 = y2 - gap - size

    return {
        insta        = UDim2.new(1, x1, 1, y1),
        drop         = UDim2.new(1, x2, 1, y1),
        autoLeft     = UDim2.new(1, x3, 1, y1),
        antiDesync   = UDim2.new(1, x1, 1, y2),
        aimbot       = UDim2.new(1, x2, 1, y2),
        autoRight    = UDim2.new(1, x3, 1, y2),
        tp           = UDim2.new(1, x2, 1, y3),
        carry        = UDim2.new(1, x3, 1, y3),
        laggerNormal = UDim2.new(1, x2, 1, y4),
        laggerCarry  = UDim2.new(1, x3, 1, y4),
    }
end

local defaults = mobileViewportDefaults()
function _G.ViperResetMobileButtons()
_G.ViperMobileButtonScale = 0.75
_G.ViperMobileButtonShape = "Circle"
_G.ViperHideMobileButtons = false
defaults = mobileViewportDefaults()
for key, defaultPos in pairs(defaults) do
local entry = mobileButtons[key]
local holder = entry and entry.holder
if holder then
holder.Position = defaultPos
holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 64)
local btn = entry.btn
if btn then
btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
btn:SetAttribute("ViperMobilePressed", false)
btn:SetAttribute("ViperMobileVisualState", nil)
end
end
end
if _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonSize then _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonSize() end
if _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonsHidden then _G.ViperApplyMobileButtonsHidden() end
if showActionNotification then pcall(function() showActionNotification("MOBILE BUTTONS RESET") end) end
saveViperConfig()
end
makeButton("insta", "INSTA\nRESET", defaults.insta, function(btn)
_G.ViperAutoResetCursedInstaReset()
pulse(btn)
end)
makeButton("drop", "DROP\nBR", defaults.drop, function(btn)
if runDropBrainrot then runDropBrainrot() elseif runDrop then runDrop() end
pulse(btn)
end)
makeButton("autoLeft", "AUTO\nLEFT", defaults.autoLeft, function(btn)
if _G.ViperSetAutoLeft then _G.ViperSetAutoLeft(not autoLeftEnabled) end
task.delay(0.03, function()
if mobileButtons.autoLeft then mobileButtons.autoLeft.setActive(autoLeftEnabled == true) end
if mobileButtons.autoRight then mobileButtons.autoRight.setActive(autoRightEnabled == true) end
end)
end)
makeButton("antiDesync", "ANTI\nDESYNC", defaults.antiDesync, function(btn)
if _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked and _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked() then
if _G.ViperSafeModeForceStop then _G.ViperSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
return
end
if _G.ViperToggleAntiDesyncAimbot then
_G.ViperToggleAntiDesyncAimbot()
elseif _G.ViperStartAntiDesyncAimbot and _G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot then
if _G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn then _G.ViperStopAntiDesyncAimbot() else _G.ViperStartAntiDesyncAimbot() end
end
task.delay(0.03, function() setActive(btn, _G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true) end)
end)
makeButton("aimbot", "BAT\nBOT", defaults.aimbot, function(btn)
if _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked and _G.ViperSafeModeIsLocked() then
if _G.ViperSafeModeForceStop then _G.ViperSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
return
end
if _G.ViperToggleSelectedAimbot then _G.ViperToggleSelectedAimbot() end
if _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.ViperRefreshAimbotVisual() end
task.delay(0.03, function()
setActive(btn, (_G.ViperNormalAimbotOn == true) or (_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn == true))
end)
end)
makeButton("autoRight", "AUTO\nRIGHT", defaults.autoRight, function(btn)
if _G.ViperSetAutoRight then _G.ViperSetAutoRight(not autoRightEnabled) end
task.delay(0.03, function()
if mobileButtons.autoRight then mobileButtons.autoRight.setActive(autoRightEnabled == true) end
if mobileButtons.autoLeft then mobileButtons.autoLeft.setActive(autoLeftEnabled == true) end
end)
end)
makeButton("tp", "TP\nDOWN", defaults.tp, function(btn)
if runTPFloor then runTPFloor() end
pulse(btn)
end)
makeButton("carry", "CARRY\nSPEED", defaults.carry, function(btn)
if setSpeedMode then setSpeedMode(currentSpeedMode == "Carry" and "Normal" or "Carry") end
task.delay(0.03, function()
if mobileButtons.carry then mobileButtons.carry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Carry") end
if mobileButtons.laggerNormal then mobileButtons.laggerNormal.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger") end
if mobileButtons.laggerCarry then mobileButtons.laggerCarry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry") end
end)
end)
makeButton("laggerNormal", "LAGGER\nNORMAL", defaults.laggerNormal, function(btn)
if setSpeedMode then setSpeedMode(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" and "Normal" or "Lagger") end
task.delay(0.03, function()
if mobileButtons.carry then mobileButtons.carry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Carry") end
if mobileButtons.laggerNormal then mobileButtons.laggerNormal.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger") end
if mobileButtons.laggerCarry then mobileButtons.laggerCarry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry") end
end)
end)
makeButton("laggerCarry", "LAGGER\nCARRY", defaults.laggerCarry, function(btn)
if setSpeedMode then setSpeedMode(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" and "Normal" or "Lagger Carry") end
task.delay(0.03, function()
if mobileButtons.carry then mobileButtons.carry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Carry") end
if mobileButtons.laggerNormal then mobileButtons.laggerNormal.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger") end
if mobileButtons.laggerCarry then mobileButtons.laggerCarry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry") end
end)
end)
_G.ViperApplyMobileButtonSize()
_G.ViperApplyMobileButtonColor()
_G.ViperApplyMobileButtonShape()
_G.ViperApplyMobileButtonsHidden()
task.spawn(function()
    while mobileGui and mobileGui.Parent do
        if mobileGui.Enabled then
            if mobileButtons.autoLeft then mobileButtons.autoLeft.setActive(autoLeftEnabled == true) end
            if mobileButtons.autoRight then mobileButtons.autoRight.setActive(autoRightEnabled == true) end
            if mobileButtons.aimbot then mobileButtons.aimbot.setActive((_G.ViperNormalAimbotOn == true) or (_G.ViperAntiBypassAimbotOn == true)) end
            if mobileButtons.antiDesync then mobileButtons.antiDesync.setActive(_G.ViperAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true) end
            if mobileButtons.carry then mobileButtons.carry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Carry") end
            if mobileButtons.laggerNormal then mobileButtons.laggerNormal.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger") end
            if mobileButtons.laggerCarry then mobileButtons.laggerCarry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry") end
        end
        task.wait(0.15)
    end
end)
end)

-- VIPER AUTO TAUNT: automatic only, no Visuals/Keybind entry.
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(120)
    while true do
        pcall(function() if _G.ViperTaunt then _G.ViperTaunt() end end)
        task.wait(120)
    end
end)
